def _namespace_from_db_path(path: Path) -> str:
    """Extract namespace name from db filename: mesh_shared.db -> shared"""
    name = path.stem
    return name[5:] if name.startswith("mesh_") else name


def _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir: Path, namespace: str) -> Path:
    """
    Resolve `mesh_<namespace>.db` inside `base_dir`, refusing symlinks on the DB or sidecars (Finding N6).
    """
    candidate = base_dir / f"mesh_{namespace}.db"
    reject_symlink(candidate)
    resolved = candidate.resolve()
    if resolved.parent != base_dir:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Refusing to operate on '{resolved}': it resolves outside the Mesh data directory."
        )
    return resolved


def _distinct_memory_namespaces(db_path: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Return the distinct `namespace` values stored in a database (empty on error)."""
    try:
        reject_symlink(db_path)
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            rows = conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT namespace FROM memories").fetchall()
        finally:
            conn.close()
        return [str(r[0]) for r in rows if r[0] is not None]
    except (sqlite3.Error, ValueError):
        return []


def _reject_namespace_collision(db_path: Path, clean_ns: str, action: str) -> None:
    """
    Refuse a destructive operation when the database's rows do not belong to `clean_ns`.
    """
    names = _distinct_memory_namespaces(db_path)
    if not names:
        return
    variants = sorted(
        {
            n
            for n in names
            if n != clean_ns and n.lower() == clean_ns.lower()
        }
    )
    if variants or clean_ns not in names:
        found = variants[0] if variants else names[0]
        raise ValueError(
            f"Cannot {action} namespace '{clean_ns}': '{db_path.name}' contains rows for "
            f"{found!r}. This filesystem is case-insensitive, so the two names share one "
            f"database. Use the exact casing ({found!r}) or migrate both casings first."
        )


def _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn: sqlite3.Connection, db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Checkpoint the WAL and fail loudly if another process prevents truncation."""
    try:
        row = conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)").fetchone()
    except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Could not checkpoint '{db_path}' ({exc}). Stop the Mesh daemon and retry."
        ) from exc
    if row and int(row[0]) != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Could not checkpoint '{db_path}' because another process is using it. "
            "Stop the Mesh daemon and other agents, then retry."
        )


def _evict_namespace_caches(namespace: str, db_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
    """
    Clear all in-memory caches and close pooled SQLite connections for `namespace`
    across store, audit, context_builder, http_server, mcp_server, and dashboard.
    Only touches already-imported server modules in `sys.modules` to preserve lazy loading.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    if db_path is None:
        base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
        db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    resolved_db = db_path.resolve()

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)

    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(resolved_db)
        _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(db_path)
        _INITIALIZED_DBS.pop(resolved_db, None)
        _INITIALIZED_DBS.pop(db_path, None)
        close_store_connection(resolved_db)
        close_store_connection(db_path)
        stale_keys = [k for k in _TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS if k[0] == clean_ns]
        for k in stale_keys:
            _TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS.pop(k, None)

    evict_audit_cache(clean_ns)

    ctx_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.context_builder")
    if ctx_mod is not None and hasattr(ctx_mod, "evict_context_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            ctx_mod.evict_context_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    http_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.http_server")
    if http_mod is not None and hasattr(http_mod, "evict_http_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            http_mod.evict_http_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    mcp_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.mcp_server")
    if mcp_mod is not None and hasattr(mcp_mod, "evict_mcp_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            mcp_mod.evict_mcp_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    dash_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.dashboard")
    if dash_mod is not None and hasattr(dash_mod, "evict_dashboard_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            dash_mod.evict_dashboard_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass


def evict_lru_namespace(exclude_namespace: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Evict the least-recently-used namespace (other than `exclude_namespace`) across
    store, vector, audit, context_builder, HTTP, MCP, and dashboard caches.
    """
    excl = validate_namespace(exclude_namespace) if exclude_namespace else None
    victim_ns: Optional[str] = None
    victim_db: Optional[Path] = None
    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        for db_path, ns in _VECTOR_CACHE.keys():
            if ns != excl:
                victim_ns = ns
                victim_db = db_path
                break
        if victim_ns is None:
            for db_path in _CONNECTION_POOL.keys():
                cand_ns = _namespace_from_db_path(db_path)
                if cand_ns != excl:
                    victim_ns = cand_ns
                    victim_db = db_path
                    break
    if victim_ns is not None:
        _evict_namespace_caches(victim_ns, victim_db)
    return victim_ns


def _remove_sqlite_files(db_path: Path, strict: bool = False) -> bool:
    """Delete a SQLite database file along with any WAL or SHM sidecar files (rejecting symlinks, Finding N6)."""
    reject_symlink(db_path)
    all_removed = True
    for suffix in ("", "-wal", "-shm"):
        candidate = Path(f"{db_path}{suffix}")
        for attempt in range(5):
            if not candidate.exists() and not candidate.is_symlink():
                break
            try:
                candidate.unlink()
                break
            except OSError:
                if attempt == 0:
                    gc.collect()
                time.sleep(0.02 * (attempt + 1))
        if candidate.exists():
            all_removed = False
            if strict:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Failed to delete SQLite file '{candidate}'. Another process may still hold the file open."
                )
    return all_removed


def list_namespaces() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    List all validated namespaces with their memory counts and last updated time.
    - Never silently drops a namespace whose DB file errors; includes it with `"error": "<message>"`.
    - Computes `last_updated` taking edits and accesses into account (`MAX(created_at, updated_at, accessed_at)`).
    """
    flush_all_telemetry()
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for db_path in _get_all_db_paths():
        namespace = _namespace_from_db_path(db_path)
        try:
            clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        try:
            reject_symlink(db_path)
            conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cols = {r[1] for r in cursor.execute("PRAGMA table_info(memories)").fetchall()}
                if not cols:
                    raise sqlite3.DatabaseError(f"Database '{db_path.name}' is missing 'memories' table")
                cursor.execute("SELECT DISTINCT namespace FROM memories")
                row_names = [str(r[0]) for r in cursor.fetchall() if r[0] is not None]
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM memories")
                total = int(cursor.fetchone()["count"] or 0)
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM memories WHERE local_only = 1")
                local_only = int(cursor.fetchone()["count"] or 0)
                if "updated_at" in cols:
                    cursor.execute(
                        """
                        SELECT MAX(
                            CASE
                                WHEN COALESCE(updated_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                     AND COALESCE(updated_at, '') >= COALESCE(accessed_at, '')
                                THEN updated_at
                                WHEN COALESCE(accessed_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                THEN accessed_at
                                ELSE created_at
                            END
                        ) as last
                        FROM memories
                        """,
                    )
                else:
                    cursor.execute(
                        """
                        SELECT MAX(
                            CASE
                                WHEN COALESCE(accessed_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                THEN accessed_at
                                ELSE created_at
                            END
                        ) as last
                        FROM memories
                        """,
                    )
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                last_updated = row["last"] if row and row["last"] else "never"
                cursor.close()
            finally:
                conn.close()
            entry: dict[str, Any] = {
                "namespace": clean_ns,
                "memory_count": total,
                "local_only_count": local_only,
                "last_updated": last_updated,
                "updated_at": last_updated,
            }
            variants = sorted({n for n in row_names if n != clean_ns})
            if variants:
                entry["namespace_variants"] = variants
            results.append(entry)
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append(
                {
                    "namespace": clean_ns,
                    "memory_count": 0,
                    "local_only_count": 0,
                    "last_updated": "error",
                    "updated_at": "error",
                    "error": str(exc),
                }
            )
    results.sort(key=lambda x: x["namespace"])
    return results


def delete_namespace(namespace: str) -> int:
    """
    Delete a validated namespace and ALL its memories (plus its isolated audit trail DB and `.lock` file).
    Raises `NamespaceNotFoundError` (a `ValueError` subclass) if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    audit_db_path = _get_audit_db_path(clean_ns)

    if not db_path.exists() and not audit_db_path.exists():
        evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{namespace}' does not exist")

    count = 0
    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_ns, db_path)

    if db_path.exists():
        reject_symlink(db_path)
        _reject_namespace_collision(db_path, clean_ns, "delete")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                count = int(row[0]) if row else 0
                cursor.close()
            except sqlite3.Error as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "Could not read memory count before deleting namespace '%s': %s",
                    clean_ns,
                    exc,
                )
                count = 0
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn, db_path)
        finally:
            conn.close()

        _evict_namespace_caches(clean_ns, db_path)

    if audit_db_path.exists():
        reject_symlink(audit_db_path)
        conn_audit = sqlite3.connect(audit_db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_audit, audit_db_path)
        finally:
            conn_audit.close()
        evict_audit_cache(clean_ns)

    if audit_db_path.exists():
        _remove_sqlite_files(audit_db_path, strict=True)
    if db_path.exists():
        _remove_sqlite_files(db_path, strict=True)

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)
    return count


def rename_namespace(old_name: str, new_name: str) -> int:
    """
    Rename a namespace safely with full validation, symlink rejection, cache eviction,
    and atomic rollback on failure.
    """
    clean_old = validate_namespace(old_name)
    clean_new = validate_namespace(new_name)
    if clean_old == clean_new:
        raise ValueError(f"Old and new namespace names are identical ('{clean_old}').")

    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    old_db = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_old)
    new_db = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_new)
    old_audit = _get_audit_db_path(clean_old)
    new_audit = _get_audit_db_path(clean_new)

    if not old_db.exists():
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{old_name}' does not exist")
    if new_db.exists() or new_audit.exists():
        raise ValueError(f"Namespace '{new_name}' already exists. Delete it first.")
    _reject_namespace_collision(old_db, clean_old, "rename")

    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_old, old_db)
    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_new, new_db)

    count = 0
    lock_names = sorted({clean_old, clean_new})
    with ExitStack() as stack:
        for lock_ns in lock_names:
            stack.enter_context(namespace_write_lock(lock_ns, timeout=30.0))

        conn_old = sqlite3.connect(old_db, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_old, old_db)
        finally:
            conn_old.close()

        try:
            reject_symlink(old_db)
            reject_symlink(new_db)
            shutil.copy2(old_db, new_db)
            secure_file_permissions(new_db)

            conn_new = sqlite3.connect(new_db, timeout=5.0)
            try:
                conn_new.execute("UPDATE memories SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old))
                conn_new.execute("UPDATE queries SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old))
                conn_new.execute(
                    "UPDATE contradictions SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old)
                )
                tables = {
                    r[0]
                    for r in conn_new.execute(
                        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
                    ).fetchall()
                }
                if "mesh_meta" in tables:
                    conn_new.execute(
                        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO mesh_meta (key, value) VALUES ('content_version', COALESCE((SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) + 1 FROM mesh_meta WHERE key = 'content_version'), '1'))"
                    )
                conn_new.commit()
                cur = conn_new.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new,)
                )
                row = cur.fetchone()
                count = int(row[0]) if row else 0
                cur.close()
            finally:
                conn_new.close()
            secure_file_permissions(new_db)

            if old_audit.exists():
                reject_symlink(old_audit)
                reject_symlink(new_audit)
                conn_audit_old = sqlite3.connect(old_audit, timeout=5.0)
                try:
                    _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_audit_old, old_audit)
                finally:
                    conn_audit_old.close()

                shutil.copy2(old_audit, new_audit)
                secure_file_permissions(new_audit)
                conn_audit_new = sqlite3.connect(new_audit, timeout=5.0)
                try:
                    conn_audit_new.execute(
                        "UPDATE audit_log SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?",
                        (clean_new, clean_old),
                    )
                    conn_audit_new.commit()
                finally:
                    conn_audit_new.close()
                secure_file_permissions(new_audit)
        except Exception:
            close_store_connection(new_db)
            _remove_sqlite_files(new_db)
            if new_audit.exists():
                _remove_sqlite_files(new_audit)
            raise

        removed = _remove_sqlite_files(old_db, strict=True)
        if old_audit.exists():
            removed = _remove_sqlite_files(old_audit, strict=True) and removed
        if not removed:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Renamed data to '{clean_new}', but could not fully remove the old namespace files. "
                "Close the Mesh daemon and retry."
            )

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_old)
    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_new)
    return count


def namespace_stats(namespace: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return detailed statistics for a validated namespace using the unified
    `_compute_row_effective_confidence()` staleness definition.
    Never creates a `.db` file if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    if not db_path.exists():
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{namespace}' does not exist")

    now = _utc_now()
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    try:
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        total = int(cursor.fetchone()["c"] or 0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? AND local_only = 1",
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        local_only = int(cursor.fetchone()["c"] or 0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT memory_type, COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? GROUP BY memory_type",
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        type_rows = cursor.fetchall()
        type_breakdown = {r["memory_type"]: int(r["c"]) for r in type_rows}

        cursor.execute("SELECT AVG(confidence) as avg FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        avg_conf_row = cursor.fetchone()
        avg_conf = float(avg_conf_row["avg"] or 0.0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT SUM(access_count) as total FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,)
        )
        recall_row = cursor.fetchone()
        total_recalls = int(recall_row["total"] or 0)

        total_searches = 0
        try:
            cursor.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM queries WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,)
            )
            total_searches = int(cursor.fetchone()["total"] or 0)
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            total_searches = 0

        cursor.execute("SELECT MIN(created_at) as oldest FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        oldest = cursor.fetchone()["oldest"] or "none"

        cursor.execute("SELECT MAX(created_at) as newest FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        newest = cursor.fetchone()["newest"] or "none"

        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT created_at, decay_start_at, ttl_days, confidence
            FROM memories
            WHERE namespace = ?
            """,
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        stale = 0
        for r in cursor.fetchall():
            _, is_stale = _compute_row_effective_confidence(dict(r), now=now)
            if is_stale:
                stale += 1

        cursor.close()
    finally:
        conn.close()

    return {
        "memory_count": total,
        "local_only_count": local_only,
        "exportable_count": total - local_only,
        "type_breakdown": type_breakdown,
        "avg_confidence": avg_conf,
        "total_recalls": total_recalls,
        "total_searches": total_searches,
        "oldest_memory": oldest,
        "newest_memory": newest,
        "stale_count": stale,
    }

=== FILE: mesh/passive.py ===
"""
mesh/passive.py — Passive memory capture from agent session logs.

Reads a conversation log (raw text, file path, directory path, or JSONL)
and extracts facts worth storing in Mesh.

Addresses Findings:
  - S2, S3, S4, S10, N2: Delegates credential detection, local redaction, cloud redaction, and
                         poison detection to `mesh.secrets` and `mesh.guards`
  - S7: Never implicitly uploads session logs to cloud providers merely because `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1`
        and an ambient API key are present; requires explicit cloud provider opt-in or dedicated
        per-invocation consent (`MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1` or interactive terminal confirmation)
  - S8: Tags passively captured memories with `trust_tier="log_captured"` and supports automatic
        quarantine (`quarantine=True` when `MESH_QUARANTINE_CAPTURE=1` or `quarantine=True` is passed)
  - N1: Confines `_resolve_log_input` to non-symlinked `.log`/`.txt`/`.jsonl`/`.md` files and
        bounded directory scans (skipping hidden/dot directories like `.ssh`, `.aws`, `.gnupg`)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from . import ui
from .audit import reject_symlink, validate_namespace
from .envutil import env_bool
from .guards import (
    INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS,
    ZERO_WIDTH_RE,
    is_poison_attempt,
)
from .memory import VALID_MEMORY_TYPES, Mesh
from .secrets import (
    is_credential,
    redact_secrets_for_cloud,
    redact_secrets_local,
)

# Backwards-compatible aliases for internal callers and existing test suites
_INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS = INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS
_ZERO_WIDTH_RE = ZERO_WIDTH_RE
_is_credential = is_credential
_is_poison_attempt = is_poison_attempt
_redact_secrets_for_cloud = redact_secrets_for_cloud
_redact_secrets_local = redact_secrets_local

EXTRACTION_PROMPT = """You are a memory extraction assistant for Mesh, a shared agent memory system.
Given a conversation or session log between a human and an AI agent, extract facts, decisions,
processes, instructions, preferences, and discoveries that would be valuable to remember for future sessions.

Rules:
- Only extract concrete, specific, reusable facts
- Do NOT extract opinions, casual conversation, or things only relevant in the moment
- Do NOT extract raw passwords, API keys, or secrets in plain text without marking them private=true
- Each memory should be a single, self-contained sentence
- Prefer specifics over generalities: "the deploy script is at ./scripts/deploy.sh" not "there is a deploy script"

Return ONLY a JSON array of objects. No preamble, no explanation, no markdown fences.
Example:
  [{"content": "the staging server URL is staging.example.com", "type": "fact", "tags": ["infrastructure"], "private": false},
   {"content": "always run tests before merging to main", "type": "instruction", "tags": ["workflow"], "private": false},
   {"content": "API key is [REDACTED_SECRET]", "type": "fact", "tags": ["credentials"], "private": true}]

Valid types: fact, preference, context, result, instruction, process, decision, error, general
If nothing worth remembering was found, return an empty array: []

Session log to analyze:
"""

_TYPE_ALIASES = {
    "rule": "instruction",
    "guideline": "instruction",
    "workflow": "process",
    "choice": "decision",
    "bug": "error",
    "fix": "result",
    "outcome": "result",
    "background": "context",
    "setting": "preference",
    "note": "general",
}

_LOG_FILE_EXTENSIONS = {".log", ".txt", ".jsonl", ".md"}
_FORBIDDEN_FILENAMES = {
    ".env", ".env.local", ".env.production", ".netrc", ".pgpass",
    "id_rsa", "id_ed25519", "id_ecdsa", "authorized_keys", "known_hosts",
    "credentials", "config", "shadow", "passwd", "master.key", "auth_token",
}
_FORBIDDEN_DIRS = {
    ".ssh", ".aws", ".gnupg", ".kube", ".docker", ".config", ".git", ".mesh",
}


def _normalize_memory_type(raw_type: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """Ensure any LLM- or heuristic-generated memory type maps cleanly to VALID_MEMORY_TYPES."""
    if not raw_type:
        return "fact"
    cleaned = str(raw_type).strip().lower()
    if cleaned in VALID_MEMORY_TYPES:
        return cleaned
    return _TYPE_ALIASES.get(cleaned, "general")


def _is_safe_log_file_path(path: Path) -> bool:
    """
    Finding N1: Verify that `path` is a non-symlinked log file with an allowed extension
    (`.log`, `.txt`, `.jsonl`, `.md`) and does not reside inside sensitive dot-directories
    (`.ssh`, `.aws`, `.gnupg`, etc.).
    """
    try:
        if path.is_symlink():
            return False
        name_low = path.name.lower()
        if name_low in _FORBIDDEN_FILENAMES or name_low.startswith(".env"):
            return False
        if path.suffix.lower() not in _LOG_FILE_EXTENSIONS:
            return False
        for part in path.parts:
            if part.lower() in _FORBIDDEN_DIRS:
                return False
        return path.is_file()
    except OSError:
        return False


def _resolve_log_input(log_input: str, max_chars: int = 16000) -> str:
    """
    Accept either raw log text OR a verified log file/directory path string.
    Finding N1: Refuses symlinks, non-log file extensions (e.g. `~/.ssh/config`, `.env`),
    and bounds directory traversal depth and file count.
    """
    if not log_input or not log_input.strip():
        return ""
    stripped = log_input.strip()
    if "\n" not in stripped and len(stripped) < 1024:
        try:
            candidate = Path(stripped).expanduser()
            if not candidate.is_symlink() and candidate.exists():
                if candidate.is_file():
                    if _is_safe_log_file_path(candidate):
                        return _read_log_file(candidate, max_chars=max_chars)
                    return stripped
                if candidate.is_dir():
                    files: list[tuple[int, Path]] = []
                    inspected = 0
                    base_depth = len(candidate.resolve().parts)
                    for root, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(candidate, followlinks=False):
                        root_path = Path(root)
                        if len(root_path.parts) - base_depth > 3:
                            dirnames[:] = []
                            continue
                        dirnames[:] = [
                            d
                            for d in dirnames
                            if not d.startswith(".") and d.lower() not in _FORBIDDEN_DIRS
                        ]
                        for fname in filenames:
                            inspected += 1
                            if inspected > 64:
                                break
                            child = root_path / fname
                            if _is_safe_log_file_path(child):
                                try:
                                    files.append((child.stat().st_mtime_ns, child))
                                except OSError:
                                    continue
                        if inspected > 64:
                            break
                    files.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
                    chunks: list[str] = []
                    total_len = 0
                    for _, fpath in files[:8]:
                        chunk = _read_log_file(fpath, max_chars=max_chars // 2)
                        if chunk:
                            chunks.append(chunk)
                            total_len += len(chunk)
                            if total_len >= max_chars:
                                break
                    chunks.reverse()
                    return "\n\n".join(chunks)
        except OSError:
            pass
    return stripped


def _jsonl_record_text(value: Any) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, list):
        parts = [_jsonl_record_text(item) for item in value]
        return "\n".join(part for part in parts if part.strip())
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key in ("text", "content", "message"):
            if key in value:
                text = _jsonl_record_text(value[key])
                if text.strip():
                    return text
    return ""


def _read_log_file(path: Path, max_chars: int = 16000) -> str:
    """Read text from a verified log or JSONL file, keeping the tail if the file is very large."""
    try:
        reject_symlink(path)
        if not _is_safe_log_file_path(path):
            return ""
        size = path.stat().st_size
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            if size > max_chars * 4:
                f.seek(max(0, size - max_chars * 4))
            raw = f.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
        if path.suffix.lower() == ".jsonl":
            extracted_lines: list[str] = []
            for line in raw.splitlines():
                line = line.strip()
                if not line:
                    continue
                try:
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                    if isinstance(obj, dict):
                        text = _jsonl_record_text(obj)
                        if text.strip():
                            extracted_lines.append(text.strip())
                            continue
                    elif isinstance(obj, str) and obj.strip():
                        extracted_lines.append(obj.strip())
                        continue
                except Exception:
                    pass
                extracted_lines.append(line)
            return "\n".join(extracted_lines)[-max_chars:]
        return raw[-max_chars:]
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return ""


def detect_provider(*, allow_implicit: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Detect the AI provider configuration.
    Finding S7: When `allow_implicit=False` (or when `MESH_NO_IMPLICIT_CLOUD=1` is set),
    ambient `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` environment variables are
    ignored unless `MESH_AI_PROVIDER` (or `MESH_AI_API_KEY` + `MESH_AI_BASE_URL`) is explicitly set.
    """
    explicit = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_PROVIDER", "").lower().strip()
    api_key_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_API_KEY", "").strip()
    model_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_MODEL", "").strip()
    base_url_override = os.environ.get("MESH_AI_BASE_URL", "").strip()

    if api_key_override and base_url_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": api_key_override,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": True,
        }

    if explicit == "anthropic":
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "anthropic",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
            "base_url": None,
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit in ("openai",):
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "openai",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "groq":
        key = api_key_override or os.environ.get("GROQ_API_KEY", "")
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "llama3-8b-8192",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
            "available": bool(key),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "ollama":
        base = base_url_override or "http://localhost:11434/v1"
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": "ollama",
            "model": model_override or "llama3",
            "base_url": base,
            "available": _ollama_is_running(base),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "openai-compatible":
        key = api_key_override or ""
        base = base_url_override or ""
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base,
            "available": bool(key and base),
            "explicit": True,
        }
    if explicit == "local":
        return {
            "provider": "local",
            "api_key": None,
            "model": None,
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": True,
        }

    # Finding S7: Do not implicitly select cloud providers when disabled or when MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1
    # is set without an explicit MESH_AI_PROVIDER (prevents silent background log exfiltration).
    if not allow_implicit or env_bool("MESH_NO_IMPLICIT_CLOUD", False) or env_bool("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", False):
        return {
            "provider": "local",
            "api_key": None,
            "model": None,
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    anthropic_key = os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "")
    if anthropic_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "anthropic",
            "api_key": anthropic_key,
            "model": model_override or "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001",
            "base_url": None,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    openai_key = os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
    if openai_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": openai_key,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    groq_key = os.environ.get("GROQ_API_KEY", "")
    if groq_key and not api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": groq_key,
            "model": model_override or "llama3-8b-8192",
            "base_url": "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    ollama_base = base_url_override or "http://localhost:11434/v1"
    if _ollama_is_running(ollama_base):
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": "ollama",
            "model": model_override or _detect_ollama_model(ollama_base),
            "base_url": ollama_base,
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    if api_key_override:
        return {
            "provider": "openai-compatible",
            "api_key": api_key_override,
            "model": model_override or "gpt-4o-mini",
            "base_url": base_url_override or "https://api.openai.com/v1",
            "available": True,
            "explicit": False,
        }

    return {
        "provider": "local",
        "api_key": None,
        "model": None,
        "base_url": None,
        "available": True,
        "explicit": False,
    }


def _request_cloud_upload_consent(provider_name: str, assume_yes: bool = False) -> bool:
    """
    Finding S7 / 2g: Cloud log upload consent that `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1` alone CANNOT grant.
    Requires:
      1. `assume_yes=True` (explicit per-invocation parameter/flag), OR
      2. `MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1` (dedicated cloud upload consent env var), OR
      3. Interactive TTY confirmation at the keyboard (with `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE` temporarily ignored).
    """
    if assume_yes or env_bool("MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE", False):
        return True
    if env_bool("MESH_NON_INTERACTIVE", False) or not ui.interactive():
        return False
    prev_auto = os.environ.pop("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE", None)
    try:
        return ui.confirm(
            f"Allow Mesh to send redacted session log text to external cloud LLM provider '{provider_name}' for memory extraction?",
            default=False,
            assume_yes=False,
        )
    finally:
        if prev_auto is not None:
            os.environ["MESH_AUTO_APPROVE"] = prev_auto


def _ollama_is_running(base_url: str) -> bool:
    try:
        import httpx

        r = httpx.get(base_url.replace("/v1", ""), timeout=1.5)
        return r.status_code < 500
    except Exception:
        return False


def _detect_ollama_model(base_url: str) -> str:
    try:
        import httpx

        r = httpx.get(base_url.replace("/v1", "/api/tags"), timeout=2.0)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            models = r.json().get("models", [])
            if models:
                return models[0]["name"]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "llama3"


def _extract_with_anthropic(log_text: str, config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    import httpx

    safe_log_text = redact_secrets_for_cloud(log_text)
    response = httpx.post(
        "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
        headers={
            "x-api-key": config["api_key"],
            "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
            "content-type": "application/json",
        },
        json={
            "model": config["model"],
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": EXTRACTION_PROMPT + safe_log_text}],
        },
        timeout=30,
    )
    if response.status_code == 401:
        raise ValueError("Invalid Anthropic API key. Check ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.")
    if response.status_code == 429:
        raise ValueError("Anthropic rate limit hit. Try again in a moment.")
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise ValueError(f"Anthropic API error {response.status_code}: {response.text[:200]}")
    raw = response.json()["content"][0]["text"].strip()
    return _parse_json_response(raw)


def _extract_with_openai_compatible(log_text: str, config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    import httpx

    safe_log_text = redact_secrets_for_cloud(log_text)
    base_url = (config.get("base_url") or "https://api.openai.com/v1").rstrip("/")
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {config['api_key']}",
    }
    response = httpx.post(
        f"{base_url}/chat/completions",
        headers=headers,
        json={
            "model": config["model"],
            "max_tokens": 1000,
            "temperature": 0.2,
            "messages": [
                {
                    "role": "system",
                    "content": "You are a memory extraction assistant. Always respond with valid JSON only.",
                },
                {"role": "user", "content": EXTRACTION_PROMPT + safe_log_text},
            ],
        },
        timeout=30,
    )
    if response.status_code == 401:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid API key for {base_url}. Check your MESH_AI_API_KEY.")
    if response.status_code == 429:
        raise ValueError("Rate limit hit. Try again in a moment.")
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise ValueError(f"API error {response.status_code} from {base_url}: {response.text[:200]}")
    raw = response.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
    return _parse_json_response(raw)


_SIGNAL_PATTERNS = [
    r"\bis at\b",
    r"\bis located at\b",
    r"\brunning on\b",
    r"\bport\s+\d+",
    r"\bURL is\b",
    r"\bpath is\b",
    r"\bscript is\b",
    r"\bcommand is\b",
    r"\balways\b",
    r"\bnever\b",
    r"\bdon't\b",
    r"\bdo not\b",
    r"\bmust\b",
    r"\bshould\b",
    r"\bwe decided\b",
    r"\bdecided to\b",
    r"\bwe use\b",
    r"\bwe're using\b",
    r"\bconfigured to\b",
    r"\bset to\b",
    r"\bdeployed on\b",
    r"\bstored in\b",
    r"\bprefers?\b",
    r"\bcredential",
    r"\bpassword\b",
    r"\bapi key\b",
    r"\btoken\b",
    r"\bsecret\b",
]

_NOISE_PATTERNS = [
    r"^\s*(yes|no|ok|okay|sure|thanks|thank you|great|got it|understood|sounds good)\s*[.!]?\s*$",
    r"^\s*okay\s+sounds\s+good\b",
    r"^\s*i\s+(see|understand|think|feel|wonder|believe)\b",
    r"^\s*(can|could|would|should)\s+you\b",
    r"^\s*please\b",
    r"^\s*here\s+(is|are)\b",
    r"^\s*let me\b",
    r"^\s*i'll\b",
]

_compiled_signals = [re.compile(p, re.IGNORECASE) for p in _SIGNAL_PATTERNS]
_compiled_noise = [re.compile(p, re.IGNORECASE) for p in _NOISE_PATTERNS]


def _is_noise(sentence: str) -> bool:
    return any(p.match(sentence) for p in _compiled_noise)


def _has_signal(sentence: str) -> bool:
    return any(p.search(sentence) for p in _compiled_signals)


def _infer_type(sentence: str) -> str:
    s = sentence.lower()
    if any(w in s for w in ["decided", "decision", "chose", "going with", "we'll use"]):
        return "decision"
    if any(w in s for w in ["prefer", "prefers", "preference"]):
        return "preference"
    if any(w in s for w in ["always", "never", "must", "should", "don't", "do not", "process", "workflow"]):
        return "process"
    if any(w in s for w in ["error", "bug", "issue", "problem", "broken", "fails", "crash"]):
        return "error"
    return "fact"


def _extract_local(log_text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    sentences = re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+|\n", log_text)
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for raw in sentences:
        sentence = raw.strip()
        if len(sentence) < 20 or len(sentence) > 300:
            continue
        if _is_noise(sentence) or is_poison_attempt(sentence):
            continue
        key = sentence.lower()[:60]
        if key in seen:
            continue
        if _has_signal(sentence):
            is_private = is_credential(sentence)
            if is_private:
                sentence = redact_secrets_local(sentence)
            results.append(
                {
                    "content": sentence,
                    "type": _infer_type(sentence),
                    "tags": ["auto-captured", "local-extraction"],
                    "private": is_private,
                }
            )
            seen.add(key)
        if len(results) >= 15:
            break
    return results


def _parse_json_response(raw: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    if "```" in raw:
        parts = raw.split("```")
        for part in parts:
            part = part.strip()
            if part.startswith("json"):
                part = part[4:].strip()
            if part.startswith("["):
                raw = part
                break
    raw = raw.strip()
    if not raw.startswith("["):
        match = re.search(r"\[.*\]", raw, re.DOTALL)
        if match:
            raw = match.group(0)
        else:
            return []
    parsed = json.loads(raw)
    if not isinstance(parsed, list):
        return []
    return [item for item in parsed if isinstance(item, dict)]


def extract_memories_from_log(
    log_text: str,
    namespace: str = "shared",
    agent_id: str = "passive-capture",
    dry_run: bool = False,
    verbose: bool = False,
    force_local: bool = False,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
    default_local_only: bool = False,
    resolve_input: bool = True,
    quarantine: Optional[bool] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Extract and store memories from a session log (or verified log file/directory path).
    Finding S7: Requires explicit cloud consent (`assume_yes=True`, `MESH_ALLOW_CLOUD_CAPTURE=1`,
    or interactive TTY prompt) that `MESH_AUTO_APPROVE=1` alone cannot grant.
    Finding S8: Marks captured memories with `trust_tier="log_captured"` and supports quarantine.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    resolved_text = _resolve_log_input(log_text) if resolve_input else str(log_text or "")
    if not resolved_text.strip():
        return {
            "captured_count": 0,
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": 0,
            "provider_used": "none",
            "memories": [],
            "error": None,
        }

    if len(resolved_text) > 8000:
        resolved_text = "[earlier content truncated]\n\n" + resolved_text[-8000:]

    if force_local:
        config: dict[str, Any] = {"provider": "local", "available": True}
    else:
        config = detect_provider()

    if config["provider"] != "local" and not config.get("available"):
        if verbose:
            ui.info(
                f"Configured provider '{config['provider']}' is unavailable — using local keyword extraction"
            )
        config = {"provider": "local", "available": True}

    # Finding S7: Require dedicated cloud consent (`MESH_AUTO_APPROVE` alone does not grant cloud upload)
    if config["provider"] != "local":
        provider_name = config["provider"]
        if not _request_cloud_upload_consent(provider_name, assume_yes=assume_yes):
            if verbose:
                ui.info("Cloud extraction declined — using local keyword extraction instead")
            config = {"provider": "local", "available": True}

    if verbose:
        if config["provider"] == "local":
            ui.info("Using local keyword extraction", "No cloud AI calls are made.")
        else:
            provider_label = config["provider"]
            model_label = config.get("model", "")
            base_label = f" ({config['base_url']})" if config.get("base_url") else ""
            ui.info(f"Using {provider_label} / {model_label}", base_label.strip(" ()") or None)

    extracted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    extraction_error: Optional[str] = None
    try:
        if config["provider"] == "anthropic":
            extracted = _extract_with_anthropic(resolved_text, config)
            provider_used = f"anthropic/{config['model']}"
        elif config["provider"] in ("openai", "openai-compatible"):
            extracted = _extract_with_openai_compatible(resolved_text, config)
            base = config.get("base_url", "")
            provider_used = f"openai-compatible/{config['model']} ({base})"
        else:
            extracted = _extract_local(resolved_text)
            provider_used = "local-keyword-extractor"
    except Exception as e:
        extraction_error = str(e)
        if verbose:
            ui.warn(f"AI extraction failed: {e}", "Falling back to local keyword extraction.")
        try:
            extracted = _extract_local(resolved_text)
            provider_used = "local-keyword-extractor (fallback)"
        except Exception as fallback_err:
            if verbose:
                ui.error(f"Local fallback also failed: {fallback_err}")
            return {
                "captured_count": 0,
                "stored_count": 0,
                "deduplicated_count": 0,
                "skipped_count": 0,
                "provider_used": "none",
                "memories": [],
                "error": extraction_error,
            }

    if not extracted:
        if verbose:
            ui.info("Nothing worth remembering was found in that log")
        return {
            "captured_count": 0,
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": 0,
            "provider_used": provider_used,
            "memories": [],
            "error": extraction_error,
        }

    if dry_run:
        ui.header("Capture preview", clean_ns, "dry run")
        ui.kv([("Would store", ui.plural(len(extracted), "memory", "memories")), ("Provider", str(provider_used))])
        print()
        for mem in extracted:
            preview_content = str(mem.get("content", ""))
            private = (
                default_local_only
                or bool(mem.get("private"))
                or is_credential(preview_content)
            )
            private_flag = "  " + ui.badge("private", "accent") if private else ""
            mtype = _normalize_memory_type(mem.get("type"))
            print(f"{ui.IND}{ui.style(ui.sym('bullet'), 'muted')} {ui.style(mtype, 'info')}{private_flag}  {' '.join(str(mem.get('content', '')).split())}")
        print()
        ui.hint("Nothing was written. Re-run without --dry-run to store these.", stream=sys.stdout)
        return {
            "captured_count": len(extracted),
            "stored_count": 0,
            "deduplicated_count": 0,
            "skipped_count": len(extracted),
            "provider_used": provider_used,
            "memories": extracted,
            "error": extraction_error,
        }

    should_quarantine = (
        env_bool("MESH_QUARANTINE_CAPTURE", False)
        if quarantine is None
        else bool(quarantine)
    )

    mesh = Mesh(namespace=clean_ns, agent_id=agent_id)
    captured = 0
    stored = 0
    deduplicated = 0
    skipped = 0
    for mem in extracted:
        content = str(mem.get("content") or "").strip()
        if not content:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        if is_poison_attempt(content):
            skipped += 1
            if verbose:
                ui.warn(f"Ignored suspicious content: {ui.truncate(content, 60)}")
            continue
        try:
            mtype = _normalize_memory_type(mem.get("type", "general"))
            raw_tags = mem.get("tags") or []
            if not isinstance(raw_tags, list):
                raw_tags = [str(raw_tags)]
            tags = list(dict.fromkeys([*(str(t) for t in raw_tags), "auto-captured"]))
            has_credential = is_credential(content)
            is_private = (
                default_local_only
                or bool(mem.get("private", False))
                or has_credential
            )
            # Finding S10: Use local redaction so non-secret emails are preserved in local DB
            if has_credential:
                content = redact_secrets_local(content)
            learn_res = mesh.learn(
                content=content,
                memory_type=mtype,
                tags=tags,
                local_only=is_private,
                confidence=0.8,
                trust_tier="log_captured",
                quarantined=should_quarantine,
            )
            captured += 1
            if learn_res.get("deduplicated"):
                deduplicated += 1
            else:
                stored += 1
            if verbose:
                private_flag = "  " + ui.badge("private", "accent") if is_private else ""
                ui.success(f"{ui.truncate(content, 80)}{private_flag}")
        except Exception as e:
            skipped += 1
            if extraction_error is None:
                extraction_error = str(e)
            if verbose:
                ui.error(f"Couldn't store \"{ui.truncate(content, 60)}\": {e}")

    return {
        "captured_count": captured,
        "stored_count": stored,
        "deduplicated_count": deduplicated,
        "skipped_count": skipped,
        "provider_used": provider_used,
        "memories": extracted,
        "quarantined": should_quarantine,
        "error": extraction_error,
    }


def capture_cmd() -> None:
    """Entry point for: mesh-capture"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Extract and store memories from a session log automatically.",
        epilog=""" Examples:   mesh-capture --file session.log   mesh-capture --file session.log --dry-run --verbose   cat output.txt | mesh-capture --stdin   mesh-capture --file session.log --local-only        """,
    )
    parser.add_argument("--file", help="Path to session log file or directory")
    parser.add_argument("--stdin", action="store_true", help="Read log from stdin")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--namespace",
        default=None,
        help="Mesh namespace (default: MESH_NAMESPACE env var or 'shared')",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="Preview what would be stored without writing anything",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--verbose",
        action="store_true",
        help="Show detailed progress and provider info",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--local-only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Force local keyword extraction, skip all AI providers",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--quarantine",
        action="store_true",
        help="Store captured memories in quarantine until approved with `mesh approve`",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--show-provider",
        action="store_true",
        help="Print which provider would be used and exit",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip interactive y/n permission prompts")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.show_provider:
        config = detect_provider()
        if config["provider"] == "local":
            ui.header("Capture provider")
            print()
            ui.kv([("Provider", "local keyword extractor"), ("AI key", ui.style("none found", "muted"))])
            ui.hint("For AI extraction set MESH_AI_PROVIDER and ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY.", stream=sys.stdout)
        else:
            ui.header("Capture provider")
            print()
            ui.kv(
                [
                    ("Provider", str(config["provider"])),
                    ("Model", str(config.get("model", "")) or None),
                    ("Base URL", str(config["base_url"]) if config.get("base_url") else None),
                ]
            )
        return

    if not args.file and not args.stdin:
        parser.print_help()
        ui.error("Tell Mesh where to read from", "Use --file <path> or pipe a log in with --stdin.")
        sys.exit(1)

    if args.stdin:
        log_text = sys.stdin.read(1_000_000)
    else:
        path = Path(args.file).expanduser()
        if not path.exists():
            ui.fail(f"File not found: {args.file}", "Check the path, or pipe the log in with --stdin.")
        if not path.is_dir() and not _is_safe_log_file_path(path):
            ui.fail(
                f"Refusing to read '{args.file}': only non-symlinked .log, .txt, .jsonl, and .md files are allowed."
            )
        log_text = _resolve_log_input(str(path))

    ns = validate_namespace(args.namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared"))
    result = extract_memories_from_log(
        log_text=log_text,
        namespace=ns,
        dry_run=args.dry_run,
        verbose=args.verbose,
        force_local=args.local_only,
        assume_yes=args.yes,
        resolve_input=False,
        quarantine=True if args.quarantine else None,
    )
    if not args.dry_run:
        provider_label = result.get("provider_used", "unknown")
        dedup_cnt = int(result.get("deduplicated_count", 0))
        detail_parts = [f"{result['skipped_count']} skipped"]
        if dedup_cnt:
            detail_parts.append(f"{dedup_cnt} deduplicated")
        detail_parts.append(f"provider: {provider_label}")
        detail_str = f"  {ui.sym('dot')}  ".join(detail_parts)
        print()
        if result["stored_count"] or dedup_cnt:
            ui.success(
                f"Captured {ui.plural(result['stored_count'], 'new memory', 'new memories')} into {ns}",
                detail_str,
            )
            ui.hint("See them with `mesh inspect`", stream=sys.stdout)
        else:
            ui.info("Nothing was stored", detail_str)

=== FILE: mesh/secrets.py ===
"""
mesh/secrets.py — First-class secret detection, redaction, locator references, and runtime injection.

Addresses Findings:
  - S1: Secret locator model (`SecretLocator`, `secret_ref`) & OS keyring / CLI resolvers + `mesh run`
  - S2: Unified detection and redaction rules (no false assurance on `was`, quoted Bearer, unclosed PEM,
        or intervening words like "the prod database password for checkout is hunter2")
  - S3: Full coverage for multi-word passphrases, short PINs/passwords, JSON (`$2b$...`, commas),
        YAML block scalars, `.env` assignments, and CLI flags (`--password`, `--token`, `--api-key`)
  - S4: Context + entropy + benign-word filtering so ordinary prose ("token limit is 4096",
        "secret santa", "The refresh token is automatically rotated", "pwd: /path") is never mangled
  - S10: Decouples local secret redaction (`redact_secrets_local`, which preserves emails) from
         cloud log scrubbing (`redact_secrets_for_cloud`, which also redacts emails)
  - N2: Bounded URL userinfo redaction that never crosses quotes/commas or port paths
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import stat
import subprocess
import threading
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from .audit import ensure_secure_mesh_dir, secure_file_permissions, validate_namespace

# Finding S8 / S10: Shared invisible character class (soft hyphen, zero-width joiners, bidi controls, tags)
INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS = (
    r"\u00ad"                  # SOFT HYPHEN
    r"\u034f"                  # COMBINING GRAPHEME JOINER
    r"\u061c"                  # ARABIC LETTER MARK
    r"\u115f\u1160"            # Hangul fillers
    r"\u17b4\u17b5"            # Khmer inherent vowels
    r"\u180b-\u180f"           # Mongolian FVS + vowel separator
    r"\u200b-\u200f"           # zero-width space .. right-to-left mark
    r"\u202a-\u202e"           # bidi embeddings / overrides
    r"\u2060-\u2064"           # word joiner .. invisible plus
    r"\u2066-\u2069"           # bidi isolates
    r"\ufeff"                  # BOM / zero-width no-break space
    r"\ufe00-\ufe0f"           # variation selectors
    r"\uffa0"                  # halfwidth Hangul filler
    r"\U000e0000-\U000e007f"   # tag characters
    r"\U000e0100-\U000e01ef"   # variation selectors supplement
)
_INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS = INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS
ZERO_WIDTH_RE = re.compile(f"[{INVISIBLE_CHAR_CLASS}]")
_ZERO_WIDTH_RE = ZERO_WIDTH_RE

# Finding S1: Supported secret locator providers and URI schemes
VALID_SECRET_PROVIDERS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "env",
        "keyring",
        "1password",
        "bitwarden",
        "sops",
        "aws_sm",
        "gcp_sm",
        "vault",
    }
)

_PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    "env": "env://",
    "keyring": "keyring://",
    "1password": "op://",
    "bitwarden": "bw://",
    "sops": "sops://",
    "aws_sm": "awssm://",
    "gcp_sm": "gcpsm://",
    "vault": "vault://",
}

_SCHEME_TO_PROVIDER: dict[str, str] = {v: k for k, v in _PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP.items()}
_VALID_KEY_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$")
_ENV_REF_ONLY_RE = re.compile(r"^\$(?:\{[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\}|[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)$")
_PLACEHOLDER_ONLY_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\[(?:REDACTED_[A-Z_]+|SECRET_REF:[^\]]+)\]$",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

# Finding S4: Nouns that follow "token", "password", "secret", or "key" to form a benign subject phrase
_BENIGN_SUBJECT_NOUNS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "limit", "limits", "count", "counts", "budget", "size", "length",
        "policy", "policies", "rotation", "rotations", "reset", "resets",
        "manager", "managers", "vault", "vaults", "expiry", "expiration",
        "ttl", "timeout", "lifetime", "header", "headers", "endpoint",
        "endpoints", "schema", "table", "column", "columns", "field",
        "fields", "format", "type", "types", "algorithm", "check",
        "validation", "validator", "window", "bucket", "rate", "quota",
        " santa", "santa", "ring", "rings", "store", "storage", "file",
        "path", "location", "variable", "env", "name", "id", "identifier",
        "hash", "hashing", "salting", "complexity", "requirement", "requirements",
        "age", "history", "reuse", "sharing", "leak", "detection", "scanner",
    }
)

# Finding S4: Benign English status/prose words that are never secret values when unquoted
_BENIGN_VALUE_WORDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "true", "false", "null", "none", "nil", "undefined", "empty",
        "required", "optional", "enabled", "disabled", "active", "inactive",
        "valid", "invalid", "expired", "revoked", "rotated", "stored",
        "saved", "kept", "located", "configured", "missing", "set", "unset",
        "redacted", "encrypted", "hashed", "masked", "hidden", "public",
        "private", "local", "automatically", "manually", "always", "never",
        "authenticated", "verified", "deprecated", "supported", "needed",
        "mandatory", "recommended", "forgotten", "changed", "updated",
        "deleted", "removed", "generated", "created", "injected", "loaded",
        "passed", "provided", "supplied", "checked", "validated", "enforced",
        "allowed", "denied", "blocked", "rejected", "accepted", "ignored",
        "used", "shared", "scoped", "locked", "unlocked", "cached", "flushed",
    }
)

# Common dictionary passwords that are all-lowercase but must still be caught in `password is <val>`
_COMMON_LOWERCASE_PASSWORDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    {
        "password", "hunter", "secret", "admin", "letmein", "qwerty",
        "welcome", "monkey", "dragon", "master", "shadow", "supersecret",
        "passphrase", "changeme", "foobar", "baseball", "football",
        "trustno1", "iloveyou", "sunshine", "princess", "whatever",
    }
)

_VAULT_LOCK = threading.RLock()


# ==============================================================================
# Secret Locator Model & Keyring / CLI Resolver (Finding S1)
# ==============================================================================


def validate_secret_key_name(key_name: str) -> str:
    """Validate a logical secret key name (e.g. 'STRIPE_SECRET_KEY' or 'db_password')."""
    if not isinstance(key_name, str):
        raise ValueError("Secret key_name must be a string.")
    clean = key_name.strip()
    if not clean or not _VALID_KEY_NAME_RE.match(clean):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid secret key_name '{key_name}'. Must match ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{{0,127}}$."
        )
    return clean


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)
class SecretLocator:
    """
    Structured pointer to where a secret lives without ever storing its plaintext value (Finding S1).
    """

    provider: str
    uri: str
    key_name: str
    description: str = ""
    namespace: str = "shared"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        prov = (self.provider or "").strip().lower()
        if prov not in VALID_SECRET_PROVIDERS:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid secret provider '{self.provider}'. Must be one of {sorted(VALID_SECRET_PROVIDERS)}."
            )
        clean_key = validate_secret_key_name(self.key_name)
        clean_uri = (self.uri or "").strip()
        expected_prefix = _PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP[prov]
        if not clean_uri.startswith(expected_prefix) or len(clean_uri) <= len(expected_prefix):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid URI '{self.uri}' for provider '{prov}'; must start with '{expected_prefix}'."
            )
        if any(ch in clean_uri for ch in ("\r", "\n", "\x00", " ", "\t")):
            raise ValueError("Secret locator URI cannot contain whitespace or control characters.")
        clean_ns = validate_namespace(self.namespace or "shared")
        object.__setattr__(self, "provider", prov)
        object.__setattr__(self, "key_name", clean_key)
        object.__setattr__(self, "uri", clean_uri)
        object.__setattr__(self, "namespace", clean_ns)
        object.__setattr__(self, "description", str(self.description or "").strip())

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "provider": self.provider,
            "uri": self.uri,
            "key_name": self.key_name,
            "namespace": self.namespace,
            "description": self.description,
        }

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), sort_keys=True)

    def format_placeholder(self) -> str:
        return f"[SECRET_REF:{self.uri}]"

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: dict[str, Any], default_namespace: str = "shared") -> "SecretLocator":
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            raise ValueError("secret_ref must be a dict or URI string.")
        uri = str(data.get("uri") or "").strip()
        provider = str(data.get("provider") or "").strip().lower()
        if not provider and uri:
            for scheme, prov in _SCHEME_TO_PROVIDER.items():
                if uri.startswith(scheme):
                    provider = prov
                    break
        key_name = str(data.get("key_name") or "").strip()
        if not key_name and uri:
            tail = uri.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].rsplit("#", 1)[-1]
            key_name = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]", "_", tail).lstrip("0123456789.-") or "SECRET_KEY"
        if not uri and provider and key_name:
            prefix = _PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP.get(provider, "")
            if provider == "keyring":
                ns = validate_namespace(str(data.get("namespace") or default_namespace))
                uri = f"keyring://mesh/{ns}/{key_name}"
            elif prefix:
                uri = f"{prefix}{key_name}"
        return cls(
            provider=provider,
            uri=uri,
            key_name=key_name,
            description=str(data.get("description") or ""),
            namespace=str(data.get("namespace") or default_namespace),
        )

    @classmethod
    def from_uri(
        cls,
        uri: str,
        key_name: Optional[str] = None,
        description: str = "",
        namespace: str = "shared",
    ) -> "SecretLocator":
        clean_uri = (uri or "").strip()
        provider = ""
        for scheme, prov in _SCHEME_TO_PROVIDER.items():
            if clean_uri.startswith(scheme):
                provider = prov
                break
        if not provider:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unrecognized secret URI scheme in '{uri}'. Expected one of {sorted(_PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP.values())}."
            )
        inferred_key = key_name
        if not inferred_key:
            tail = clean_uri.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].rsplit("#", 1)[-1]
            inferred_key = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]", "_", tail).lstrip("0123456789.-") or "SECRET_KEY"
        return cls(
            provider=provider,
            uri=clean_uri,
            key_name=inferred_key,
            description=description,
            namespace=namespace,
        )


def normalize_secret_ref(
    secret_ref: Optional[SecretLocator | dict[str, Any] | str],
    default_namespace: str = "shared",
) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
    """Validate and normalize a `secret_ref` input into a canonical dictionary."""
    if secret_ref is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(secret_ref, SecretLocator):
        return secret_ref.to_dict()
    if isinstance(secret_ref, str):
        s = secret_ref.strip()
        if not s:
            return None
        if s.startswith("{"):
            parsed = json.loads(s)
            return SecretLocator.from_dict(parsed, default_namespace=default_namespace).to_dict()
        return SecretLocator.from_uri(s, namespace=default_namespace).to_dict()
    if isinstance(secret_ref, dict):
        return SecretLocator.from_dict(secret_ref, default_namespace=default_namespace).to_dict()
    raise ValueError("Invalid secret_ref: expected SecretLocator, dict, or URI string.")


# ==============================================================================
# Local Vault & OS Keyring Storage (Finding S1, 2a, 2b)
# ==============================================================================


def _local_vault_path() -> Path:
    return ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve() / "keyring_vault.json"


def _use_isolated_local_vault() -> bool:
    """
    When `MESH_HOME` / `MESH_DIR` is overridden (e.g. in tests or isolated workspaces)
    or `MESH_KEYRING_BACKEND=local` is set, use the isolated local vault inside `get_mesh_dir()`
    so tests never pollute the host OS keychain.
    """
    if os.environ.get("MESH_KEYRING_BACKEND", "").strip().lower() == "local":
        return True
    if os.environ.get("MESH_HOME") or os.environ.get("MESH_DIR"):
        return True
    return False


def _read_local_vault_locked() -> dict[str, str]:
    vault_file = _local_vault_path()
    if not vault_file.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        raw = vault_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        try:
            from .crypto import decrypt_text_if_encrypted

            raw = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(raw)
        except Exception:
            pass
        data = json.loads(raw)
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in data.items()}
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {}


def _write_local_vault_locked(data: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    vault_file = _local_vault_path()
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(vault_file.parent)
    serialized = json.dumps(data, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
    try:
        from .crypto import encrypt_text_for_storage, is_encryption_enabled

        if is_encryption_enabled():
            serialized = encrypt_text_for_storage(serialized)
    except Exception:
        pass
    tmp_path = vault_file.with_suffix(f".tmp.{os.getpid()}.{threading.get_ident()}")
    if os.name == "nt":
        tmp_path.write_text(serialized, encoding="utf-8")
        os.replace(tmp_path, vault_file)
        secure_file_permissions(vault_file)
        return
    flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC
    if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
        flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
    fd = os.open(str(tmp_path), flags, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(serialized)
    os.replace(tmp_path, vault_file)
    secure_file_permissions(vault_file)


def store_secret_in_keyring(
    namespace: str,
    key_name: str,
    secret_value: str,
    description: str = "",
) -> SecretLocator:
    """
    Store `secret_value` in the OS keyring (or isolated local vault fallback) and return
    a `SecretLocator` pointing to `keyring://mesh/<namespace>/<key_name>` (Finding S1).
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    clean_key = validate_secret_key_name(key_name)
    if not secret_value:
        raise ValueError("secret_value cannot be empty.")

    service_name = f"mesh:{clean_ns}"
    stored_in_os = False

    if not _use_isolated_local_vault():
        try:
            import keyring  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

            keyring.set_password(service_name, clean_key, secret_value)
            stored_in_os = True
        except Exception:
            stored_in_os = False

    if not stored_in_os:
        with _VAULT_LOCK:
            vault = _read_local_vault_locked()
            vault[f"{clean_ns}:{clean_key}"] = secret_value
            _write_local_vault_locked(vault)

    uri = f"keyring://mesh/{clean_ns}/{clean_key}"
    return SecretLocator(
        provider="keyring",
        uri=uri,
        key_name=clean_key,
        description=description or f"Secret '{clean_key}' stored in keyring for namespace '{clean_ns}'",
        namespace=clean_ns,
    )


def get_secret_from_keyring(namespace: str, key_name: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """Retrieve a secret value from the OS keyring or local vault fallback."""
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    clean_key = validate_secret_key_name(key_name)
    service_name = f"mesh:{clean_ns}"

    if not _use_isolated_local_vault():
        try:
            import keyring  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

            val = keyring.get_password(service_name, clean_key)
            if val is not None:
                return str(val)
        except Exception:
            pass

    with _VAULT_LOCK:
        vault = _read_local_vault_locked()
        return vault.get(f"{clean_ns}:{clean_key}")


class SecretResolver:
    """
    Resolves a `SecretLocator` to its runtime secret value for local CLI execution (`mesh run`).
    Never exposed to MCP tools or HTTP API responses (Finding S1).
    """

    @staticmethod
    def resolve(locator: SecretLocator | dict[str, Any] | str) -> str:
        if isinstance(locator, str):
            loc = SecretLocator.from_uri(locator)
        elif isinstance(locator, dict):
            loc = SecretLocator.from_dict(locator)
        elif isinstance(locator, SecretLocator):
            loc = locator
        else:
            raise ValueError("Invalid secret locator.")

        prov = loc.provider
        uri = loc.uri

        if prov == "env":
            var_name = uri[len("env://") :].strip()
            val = os.environ.get(var_name)
            if val is None:
                raise KeyError(f"Environment variable '{var_name}' is not set.")
            return val

        if prov == "keyring":
            # Format: keyring://mesh/<namespace>/<key_name> or keyring://<service>/<key_name>
            rest = uri[len("keyring://") :]
            parts = [p for p in rest.split("/") if p]
            if len(parts) >= 3 and parts[0] == "mesh":
                ns, key = parts[1], parts[2]
            elif len(parts) == 2:
                ns, key = parts[0], parts[1]
            else:
                ns, key = loc.namespace, loc.key_name
            val = get_secret_from_keyring(ns, key)
            if val is None:
                raise KeyError(f"Secret '{key}' not found in keyring for namespace '{ns}'.")
            return val

        if prov == "1password":
            res = subprocess.run(
                ["op", "read", uri],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"1Password CLI ('op read') failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        if prov == "bitwarden":
            item_id = uri[len("bw://") :].strip()
            res = subprocess.run(
                ["bw", "get", "password", item_id],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Bitwarden CLI ('bw get password') failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        if prov == "sops":
            raw_target = uri[len("sops://") :]
            file_part, _, key_part = raw_target.partition("#")
            cmd = ["sops", "-d"]
            if key_part:
                cmd.extend(["--extract", f'["{key_part}"]'])
            cmd.append(file_part)
            res = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"SOPS CLI failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        if prov == "aws_sm":
            secret_id = uri[len("awssm://") :].strip()
            res = subprocess.run(
                [
                    "aws",
                    "secretsmanager",
                    "get-secret-value",
                    "--secret-id",
                    secret_id,
                    "--query",
                    "SecretString",
                    "--output",
                    "text",
                ],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"AWS Secrets Manager CLI failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        if prov == "gcp_sm":
            secret_name = uri[len("gcpsm://") :].strip()
            res = subprocess.run(
                ["gcloud", "secrets", "versions", "access", "latest", f"--secret={secret_name}"],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"GCP Secret Manager CLI failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        if prov == "vault":
            vault_path = uri[len("vault://") :].strip()
            path_part, _, field_part = vault_path.partition("#")
            field_name = field_part or "value"
            res = subprocess.run(
                ["vault", "kv", "get", f"-field={field_name}", path_part],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=15.0,
                check=False,
            )
            if res.returncode != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"HashiCorp Vault CLI failed with exit code {res.returncode}.")
            return res.stdout.rstrip("\r\n")

        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported secret provider '{prov}'.")


# ==============================================================================
# Value-Based, Contextual & Entropy Secret Detector (Findings S2, S3, S4, N2)
# ==============================================================================


def _shannon_entropy(s: str) -> float:
    """Compute Shannon entropy in bits per character."""
    if not s:
        return 0.0
    length = len(s)
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for ch in s:
        counts[ch] = counts.get(ch, 0) + 1
    return -sum((c / length) * math.log2(c / length) for c in counts.values())


def _is_filesystem_path(val: str) -> bool:
    v = val.strip().strip("'\"")
    if v.startswith(("/", "./", "../", "~/")):
        return True
    if len(v) >= 3 and v[1] == ":" and v[2] in ("\\", "/"):
        return True
    return False


def _is_plausible_secret_value(
    raw_value: str,
    operator: str,
    keyword: str,
    was_quoted: bool = False,
) -> bool:
    """
    Determine whether `raw_value` captured after `keyword` and `operator` is a real secret
    rather than ordinary English prose (Findings S2, S3, S4).
    """
    val = raw_value.strip()
    if not val:
        return False

    # Never flag existing redaction or secret_ref placeholders or shell variable references ($VAR / ${VAR})
    if _PLACEHOLDER_ONLY_RE.match(val) or _ENV_REF_ONLY_RE.match(val):
        return False
    if val.startswith(("[REDACTED_", "[SECRET_REF:")):
        return False

    kw_low = keyword.strip().lower()
    op_low = operator.strip().lower()

    # Finding S4: `pwd: /Users/alice/...` is a working directory path, not a password
    if kw_low == "pwd" and _is_filesystem_path(val):
        return False

    val_low = val.lower().rstrip(".,;!?")
    if not was_quoted and val_low in _BENIGN_VALUE_WORDS:
        return False

    # Quoted values (e.g. "correct horse battery staple", "$2b$12$...", "p@ss,word;123", "hunter2")
    if was_quoted:
        if len(val) < 2:
            return False
        if val_low in _BENIGN_VALUE_WORDS:
            return False
        if kw_low == "pwd" and _is_filesystem_path(val):
            return False
        return True

    # CLI flags (--password x, --token y, --api-key z)
    if op_low.startswith("-"):
        return len(val) >= 2 and val_low not in _BENIGN_VALUE_WORDS
