    # Short PINs / passwords with explicit `=` or `:`
    if op_low in ("=", ":", ":="):
        if len(val) < 3:
            return False
        # Purely lowercase English word after `:` (e.g., "password: required", "token: bearer")
        if op_low == ":" and val.isalpha() and val.islower() and val_low not in _COMMON_LOWERCASE_PASSWORDS:
            if _shannon_entropy(val) < 3.5:
                return False
        return True

    # Natural-language copula (`is`, `was`, `equals`)
    if len(val) < 3:
        return False
    if val_low in _COMMON_LOWERCASE_PASSWORDS:
        return True
    has_digit = any(c.isdigit() for c in val)
    has_symbol = any(not c.isalnum() for c in val)
    has_non_underscore_symbol = any(not c.isalnum() and c != "_" for c in val)
    has_mixed_case = any(c.isupper() for c in val) and any(c.islower() for c in val)

    # For generic 'key' (not api_key, private_key, secret_key), an underscore alone without digits or mixed case
    # is often a database identifier (e.g. 'foreign key is customer_id'). Require digits, mixed case, non-underscore symbol, or high entropy.
    if kw_low.endswith("key") and not any(kw_low.endswith(k) for k in ("api_key", "secret_key", "private_key", "access_key", "webhook_key")):
        if has_digit or has_non_underscore_symbol or has_mixed_case:
            return True
        if len(val) >= 14 and _shannon_entropy(val) >= 3.4:
            return True
        return False

    if kw_low.endswith("code"):
        if any(kw_low.endswith(k) for k in ("status_code", "status code", "response code", "http code", "exit code", "error code", "area code", "zip code", "country code", "postal code")):
            return False
        if has_digit or has_symbol or has_mixed_case:
            return True
        if len(val) >= 14 and _shannon_entropy(val) >= 3.4:
            return True
        return False

    if has_digit or has_symbol or has_mixed_case:
        # Exclude pure version/port numbers if keyword wasn't password/pin/pwd/secret
        return True
    if len(val) >= 14 and _shannon_entropy(val) >= 3.55:
        return True
    return False


# Tier 1: Context-free well-known credential / token / key patterns (Findings S2, S3, N2)
_PEM_BLOCK_RE = re.compile(
    r"-----BEGIN [A-Z0-9 ]*PRIVATE KEY-----(?:[\s\S]*?-----END [A-Z0-9 ]*PRIVATE KEY-----|[^\r\n]*)"
)
_JWT_RE = re.compile(r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\.eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\b")
_KNOWN_API_KEY_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:"
    r"sk-ant-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{8,}|"
    r"sk-proj-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{12,}|"
    r"sk-svcacct-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{12,}|"
    r"sk-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{6,}|"
    r"gh[pousr]_[a-zA-Z0-9]{12,}|"
    r"github_pat_[a-zA-Z0-9_]{16,}|"
    r"glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{12,}|"
    r"hf_[a-zA-Z0-9]{16,}|"
    r"AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}|"
    r"ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}|"
    r"xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]{10,}|"
    r"AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{20,}|"
    r"rk_(?:live|test)_[0-9a-zA-Z]{12,}|"
    r"sk_(?:live|test)_[0-9a-zA-Z]{12,}|"
    r"pypi-AgEIcHlwaS5vcmc[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}|"
    r"npm_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}|"
    r"SG\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}|"
    r"DEMO_FAKE_[A-Za-z0-9_]+|"
    r"(?:[A-Z0-9]+_)+(?:KEY|SECRET|TOKEN|PASS|PHRASE|PASSPHRASE|APIKEY|AUTH|CRED|EMBED)_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{4,}|"
    r"(?:api[_-]?key|secret[_-]?key|access[_-]?token|auth[_-]?token)_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,}"
    r")\b"
)

# Finding S2: Authorization / Bearer headers supporting optional quotes around the token
_AUTH_HEADER_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)\b(authorization\s*[:=]\s*(?:basic|bearer|token)\s+)(['\"]?)(?!\[(?:REDACTED|SECRET_REF))([^\s'\"`;\n]{4,})\2"
)
_BEARER_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)\b(Bearer\s+)(['\"]?)(?!\[(?:REDACTED|SECRET_REF))([A-Za-z0-9_\-./+=]{8,})\2"
)

# Finding N2: URL userinfo credentials bounded so they never cross quotes/commas or match `localhost:8080/v1`
_URL_USERINFO_RE = re.compile(
    r"([a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9+.-]{0,30}://[^/\s:@\"'`,;<>]*:)"
    r"(?![0-9]{1,5}(?:[/\"'`,;\s]|$))"
    r"(?!\[REDACTED)"
    r"([^\s\"'`,;<>]{1,256})"
    r"(@[A-Za-z0-9._\[\]-]+)"
)

# Finding S3: CLI flags (`--password x`, `--token y`, `--api-key z`, `--secret w`, `--client-secret v`)
_CLI_SECRET_FLAG_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)(--(?:password|passwd|passphrase|token|api-key|apikey|secret|client-secret|access-token|auth-token|private-key))"
    r"(\s*=\s*|\s+)"
    r"(?:(['\"])([^\r\n]+?)\3|([^\s'\"`;\n]{3,}))"
)

# Finding S3: YAML multiline block scalar (`password: |\n  secret_line`)
_YAML_BLOCK_SECRET_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)\b(password|passwd|passphrase|secret|api[_\s-]?key|private[_\s-]?key|access[_\s-]?token|auth[_\s-]?token|token)"
    r"(\s*:\s*[|>][-+]?[ \t]*\r?\n)"
    r"((?:[ \t]+[^\r\n]+(?:\r?\n|$))+)"
)

# Finding S2 / S3 / S4: Keyword + optional 0..5 context words (`for checkout`) + operator (`is`/`was`/`=`/`:`) + quoted or unquoted value
_KEYWORD_ASSIGNMENT_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)(?:\b|(?<=_))(password|passwd|passphrase|pwd|pin|secret|client[_\s-]?secret|"
    r"(?:[a-z0-9_-]+[_\s-])?code|passcode|"
    r"aws_secret_access_key|aws_session_token|(?:[a-z0-9_-]+[_\s-])?key|(?:[a-z0-9_-]+[_\s-])?token)\b"
    r"(\s+(?:(?:for|of|on|in|to|at|from|the|a|an|our|my|their|this|that)\s+|[a-z0-9_.-]+\s+){0,5}?"
    r"(?:is|was|equals)\s+|['\"]?\s*(?::=|=|:)\s*)"
    r"(?:(['\"])([^\r\n]*?)\3|([^\s,'\"`;\n\r\[]{3,}))"
)

# Unquoted multi-word passphrase (`passphrase is correct horse battery staple`)
_UNQUOTED_PASSPHRASE_RE = re.compile(
    r"(?i)\b(passphrase|recovery[_\s-]?phrase|seed[_\s-]?phrase|mnemonic)\b"
    r"(\s*(?:is|was|=|:)\s*)"
    r"(?!\[REDACTED)([A-Za-z0-9_]+(?:[\s_-]+[A-Za-z0-9_]+){1,11})"
)

_EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r"\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}\b")



def _context_has_benign_subject_noun(intervening_text: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if the words between the keyword and operator form a benign phrase like 'token limit is'."""
    words = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9_]+", intervening_text.lower())
    # Strip trailing operator word ('is', 'was', 'equals')
    if words and words[-1] in ("is", "was", "equals"):
        words = words[:-1]
    if not words:
        return False
    # If there is no preposition ('for', 'of', 'on', 'in', 'to', 'at') and the noun is in _BENIGN_SUBJECT_NOUNS
    prepositions = {"for", "of", "on", "in", "to", "at", "from"}
    if not (set(words) & prepositions) and any(w in _BENIGN_SUBJECT_NOUNS for w in words):
        return True
    return False


def _normalize_for_detection(text: str) -> str:
    cleaned = ZERO_WIDTH_RE.sub("", str(text or ""))
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", cleaned)


def is_credential(text: str) -> bool:
    """
    Return True if `text` contains a live credential, API key, private key, JWT,
    URL password, CLI secret flag, YAML block secret, or secret assignment (Findings S2, S3, S4).
    """
    norm = _normalize_for_detection(text)
    if not norm:
        return False

    if _PEM_BLOCK_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    if _JWT_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    if _KNOWN_API_KEY_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    if _URL_USERINFO_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    if _AUTH_HEADER_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    if _BEARER_TOKEN_RE.search(norm):
        return True
    for m in _YAML_BLOCK_SECRET_RE.finditer(norm):
        yaml_content = m.group(3).strip()
        if not yaml_content.startswith(("[REDACTED", "[SECRET_REF:")):
            return True
    if _UNQUOTED_PASSPHRASE_RE.search(norm):
        return True

    for m in _CLI_SECRET_FLAG_RE.finditer(norm):
        flag_name = m.group(1)
        was_quoted = m.group(3) is not None
        val = m.group(4) if was_quoted else (m.group(5) or "")
        if _is_plausible_secret_value(val, flag_name, flag_name.lstrip("-"), was_quoted=was_quoted):
            return True

    for m in _KEYWORD_ASSIGNMENT_RE.finditer(norm):
        kw = m.group(1)
        mid = m.group(2)
        if _context_has_benign_subject_noun(mid):
            continue
        op_match = re.search(r"(?i)(is|was|equals|:=|=|:)\s*$", mid)
        op = op_match.group(1) if op_match else "="
        was_quoted = m.group(3) is not None
        val = m.group(4) if was_quoted else (m.group(5) or "")
        if _is_plausible_secret_value(val, op, kw, was_quoted=was_quoted):
            return True

    return False


def _redact_common_secrets(
    text: str,
    replacement_fn: Optional[Callable[[str, str], str]] = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Apply all secret redaction rules consistently across `text`.
    If `replacement_fn(secret_type, raw_secret_value)` is provided (used by convert-to-secret_ref),
    replaces the secret with the returned locator placeholder instead of `[REDACTED_<TYPE>]`.
    """
    redacted = _normalize_for_detection(text)

    def _rep(secret_type: str, raw_val: str, default_placeholder: str) -> str:
        if replacement_fn is not None:
            return replacement_fn(secret_type, raw_val)
        return default_placeholder

    # 1. PEM private keys (closed or unclosed)
    def _sub_pem(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        full = m.group(0)
        return _rep("private_key", full, "[REDACTED_PRIVATE_KEY]")

    redacted = _PEM_BLOCK_RE.sub(_sub_pem, redacted)

    # 2. JWTs
    redacted = _JWT_RE.sub(
        lambda m: _rep("jwt", m.group(0), "[REDACTED_JWT]"),
        redacted,
    )

    # 3. Known API key prefixes
    redacted = _KNOWN_API_KEY_RE.sub(
        lambda m: _rep("api_key", m.group(0), "[REDACTED_API_KEY]"),
        redacted,
    )

    # 4. Authorization headers (Basic / Bearer / Token, quoted or unquoted)
    def _sub_auth_hdr(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        prefix, quote, val = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        if val.startswith("[REDACTED") or val.startswith("[SECRET_REF:"):
            return m.group(0)
        sub_val = _rep("token", val, "[REDACTED_TOKEN]")
        return f"{prefix}{quote}{sub_val}{quote}"

    redacted = _AUTH_HEADER_RE.sub(_sub_auth_hdr, redacted)

    # 5. Standalone Bearer tokens (quoted or unquoted)
    def _sub_bearer(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        prefix, quote, val = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        if val.startswith("[REDACTED") or val.startswith("[SECRET_REF:"):
            return m.group(0)
        sub_val = _rep("token", val, "[REDACTED_TOKEN]")
        return f"{prefix}{quote}{sub_val}{quote}"

    redacted = _BEARER_TOKEN_RE.sub(_sub_bearer, redacted)

    # 6. URL userinfo passwords (bounded, Finding N2)
    def _sub_url_pw(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        pre, pw, host = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        if pw.startswith("[REDACTED") or pw.startswith("[SECRET_REF:"):
            return m.group(0)
        sub_val = _rep("password", pw, "[REDACTED_PASSWORD]")
        return f"{pre}{sub_val}{host}"

    redacted = _URL_USERINFO_RE.sub(_sub_url_pw, redacted)

    # 7. YAML multiline block scalars (Finding S3)
    def _sub_yaml_block(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        kw, sep, block = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        raw_secret = "\n".join(line.strip() for line in block.splitlines() if line.strip())
        first_indent_match = re.match(r"([ \t]+)", block)
        indent = first_indent_match.group(1) if first_indent_match else "  "
        trailing_nl = "\n" if block.endswith("\n") else ""
        sub_val = _rep(kw.lower(), raw_secret, "[REDACTED_SECRET]")
        return f"{kw}{sep}{indent}{sub_val}{trailing_nl}"

    redacted = _YAML_BLOCK_SECRET_RE.sub(_sub_yaml_block, redacted)

    # 8. Unquoted multi-word passphrases (Finding S3)
    def _sub_passphrase(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        kw, sep, phrase = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
        sub_val = _rep("passphrase", phrase, "[REDACTED_SECRET]")
        return f"{kw}{sep}{sub_val}"

    redacted = _UNQUOTED_PASSPHRASE_RE.sub(_sub_passphrase, redacted)

    # 9. CLI flags (--password, --token, --api-key, etc., Finding S3)
    def _sub_cli_flag(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        flag_name, sep, quote = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3) or ""
        was_quoted = m.group(3) is not None
        val = m.group(4) if was_quoted else (m.group(5) or "")
        if not _is_plausible_secret_value(val, flag_name, flag_name.lstrip("-"), was_quoted=was_quoted):
            return m.group(0)
        sub_val = _rep(flag_name.lstrip("-").lower(), val, "[REDACTED_SECRET]")
        return f"{flag_name}{sep}{quote}{sub_val}{quote}"

    redacted = _CLI_SECRET_FLAG_RE.sub(_sub_cli_flag, redacted)

    # 10. Keyword assignments & natural language (`is`/`was`/`=`/`:`, Findings S2, S3, S4)
    def _sub_kw_assign(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:
        kw, mid, quote = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3) or ""
        if _context_has_benign_subject_noun(mid):
            return m.group(0)
        op_match = re.search(r"(?i)(is|was|equals|:=|=|:)\s*$", mid)
        op = op_match.group(1) if op_match else "="
        was_quoted = m.group(3) is not None
        val = m.group(4) if was_quoted else (m.group(5) or "")
        if not _is_plausible_secret_value(val, op, kw, was_quoted=was_quoted):
            return m.group(0)
        # Preserve trailing sentence punctuation on unquoted natural-language tokens
        trailing_punct = ""
        clean_val = val
        if not was_quoted and op.lower() in ("is", "was", "equals") and clean_val and clean_val[-1] in ".,;!?":
            trailing_punct = clean_val[-1]
            clean_val = clean_val[:-1]
        sub_val = _rep(kw.lower(), clean_val, "[REDACTED_SECRET]")
        return f"{kw}{mid}{quote}{sub_val}{quote}{trailing_punct}"

    redacted = _KEYWORD_ASSIGNMENT_RE.sub(_sub_kw_assign, redacted)
    return redacted


def redact_secrets_local(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Redact credentials, API keys, tokens, and passwords for local storage.
    Deliberately preserves email addresses so local operational notes like
    'the admin contact alice@example.com is in the runbook' remain intact (Finding S10).
    """
    return _redact_common_secrets(text)


def redact_secrets_for_cloud(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Redact all credentials, API keys, tokens, passwords, AND email addresses
    before transmitting session log text to any external cloud LLM provider (Finding S10).
    """
    redacted = _redact_common_secrets(text)
    redacted = _EMAIL_RE.sub("[REDACTED_EMAIL]", redacted)
    return redacted


def convert_text_secrets_to_refs(
    text: str,
    namespace: str = "shared",
    key_name_hint: Optional[str] = None,
) -> tuple[str, list[SecretLocator]]:
    """
    Convert-instead-of-destroy helper (Finding S1 / 2b):
    Vaults every detected secret value in `text` into the OS keyring (or local vault fallback),
    replaces the secret in `text` with `[SECRET_REF:keyring://mesh/<ns>/<key>]`,
    and returns `(converted_text, list_of_locators)`.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    locators: list[SecretLocator] = []
    counter = 0

    def _vault_and_replace(secret_type: str, raw_val: str) -> str:
        nonlocal counter
        counter += 1
        if key_name_hint and counter == 1:
            base_key = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_.-]", "_", key_name_hint).lstrip("0123456789.-") or "SECRET"
        else:
            clean_type = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_]", "_", secret_type).upper().strip("_") or "SECRET"
            digest = hashlib.sha256(raw_val.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8]
            base_key = f"{clean_type}_{digest}"
        loc = store_secret_in_keyring(
            namespace=clean_ns,
            key_name=base_key,
            secret_value=raw_val,
            description=f"Auto-vaulted {secret_type} from namespace '{clean_ns}'",
        )
        locators.append(loc)
        return loc.format_placeholder()

    converted = _redact_common_secrets(text, replacement_fn=_vault_and_replace)
    return converted, locators


def run_with_secrets(
    command: list[str],
    namespace: str = "shared",
    env_specs: Optional[list[str]] = None,
) -> int:
    """
    Resolve `secret_ref` memories in `namespace` (or explicit `ENV_VAR=URI` / `KEY_NAME` specs)
    and execute `command` with the resolved secrets injected into the child environment (Finding S1 / 2a).
    Never logs or prints any secret value.
    """
    if not command:
        raise ValueError("No command specified for `mesh run`.")
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    child_env = os.environ.copy()

    # 1. Load secret_ref memories from the namespace if it exists on disk
    from .store import MeshStore, NamespaceNotFoundError

    stored_locators: dict[str, SecretLocator] = {}
    try:
        store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
        for row in store.list_all(limit=500, memory_type="secret_ref", include_private=True):
            raw_ref = row.get("secret_ref")
            if raw_ref:
                try:
                    ref_dict = json.loads(raw_ref) if isinstance(raw_ref, str) else raw_ref
                    loc = SecretLocator.from_dict(ref_dict, default_namespace=clean_ns)
                    stored_locators[loc.key_name] = loc
                except Exception:
                    continue
    except NamespaceNotFoundError:
        pass

    # 2. Resolve requested env_specs (or all secret_ref memories in namespace if env_specs omitted)
    if env_specs:
        for spec in env_specs:
            clean_spec = spec.strip()
            if not clean_spec:
                continue
            if "=" in clean_spec:
                env_var, target = clean_spec.split("=", 1)
                env_var = validate_secret_key_name(env_var)
                target = target.strip()
                if any(target.startswith(s) for s in _PROVIDER_SCHEME_MAP.values()):
                    loc = SecretLocator.from_uri(target, key_name=env_var, namespace=clean_ns)
                elif target in stored_locators:
                    loc = stored_locators[target]
                else:
                    loc = SecretLocator(
                        provider="keyring",
                        uri=f"keyring://mesh/{clean_ns}/{target}",
                        key_name=env_var,
                        namespace=clean_ns,
                    )
                child_env[env_var] = SecretResolver.resolve(loc)
            else:
                key_name = validate_secret_key_name(clean_spec)
                loc = stored_locators.get(key_name) or SecretLocator(
                    provider="keyring",
                    uri=f"keyring://mesh/{clean_ns}/{key_name}",
                    key_name=key_name,
                    namespace=clean_ns,
                )
                child_env[key_name] = SecretResolver.resolve(loc)
    else:
        for key_name, loc in stored_locators.items():
            child_env[key_name] = SecretResolver.resolve(loc)

    proc = subprocess.run(command, env=child_env, shell=False, check=False)
    return int(proc.returncode)


# Legacy underscore aliases for internal/test backwards compatibility
_is_credential = is_credential
_redact_secrets_for_cloud = redact_secrets_for_cloud
_redact_secrets_local = redact_secrets_local

=== FILE: mesh/skill_installer.py ===
"""
mesh/skill_installer.py — Agent skill installer for mesh-context-layer.

Copies the bundled mesh-context-layer SKILL.md (and any sibling files) into
every AI coding-agent skill folder detected on this machine.

Supported agents (verified global skill directories):
  - Antigravity / Gemini CLI IDE   ~/.gemini/config/skills/
  - Gemini CLI                     ~/.gemini/skills/
  - Claude Code                    ~/.claude/skills/
  - Cursor                         ~/.cursor/skills/
                                   %APPDATA%/Cursor/User/skills/ (Win fallback)
                                   ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/skills/ (macOS fallback)
                                   ~/.config/cursor/User/skills/ (Linux fallback)
  - OpenAI Codex CLI               ~/.codex/skills/
  - OpenCode                       ~/.config/opencode/skills/
  - Windsurf (Cascade)             ~/.codeium/windsurf/skills/
  - Generic .agents standard       ~/.agents/skills/
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from .version import VERSION

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Bundled skill source — lives next to this file inside the installed package
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_SKILL_NAME = "mesh-context-layer"
_SKILLS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "skills"
_VERSION_MARKER_FILE = ".mesh-skill-version"


def _skill_source() -> Path:
    """Return the path to the bundled mesh-context-layer skill directory."""
    src = _SKILLS_DIR / _SKILL_NAME
    if not src.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Bundled skill directory not found: {src}\n"
            "This usually means mesh-context-layer was not installed correctly.\n"
            "Try: pip install --force-reinstall mesh-context-layer"
        )
    return src


def _is_symlink_or_junction(path: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True if `path` is a POSIX/Windows symlink or a Windows NTFS junction."""
    if path.is_symlink():
        return True
    if hasattr(path, "is_junction"):
        try:
            if path.is_junction():  # Python 3.12+
                return True
        except OSError:
            pass
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            st = path.lstat()
            # FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT == 0x400
            if getattr(st, "st_file_attributes", 0) & 0x400:
                return True
        except OSError:
            pass
    return False


def _remove_path_or_link(path: Path) -> None:
    """Safely remove a directory, symlink, or Windows junction."""
    if _is_symlink_or_junction(path):
        try:
            path.unlink()
            return
        except OSError:
            if os.name == "nt":
                os.rmdir(path)
                return
            raise
    if path.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    elif path.exists():
        path.unlink()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Agent skill directory resolution
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _candidate_skill_roots() -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    """
    Return a deduplicated list of `(agent_label, skills_root_path)` tuples for
    every AI coding agent detected on this machine. Only includes directories
    whose *parent* already exists (we don't create new `~/.config/` trees).
    """
    home = Path.home()
    appdata_raw = os.environ.get("APPDATA", "").strip() if os.name == "nt" else ""
    appdata = Path(appdata_raw) if appdata_raw else None
    xdg_raw = os.environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", "").strip()
    xdg_config = Path(xdg_raw) if xdg_raw else (home / ".config")

    candidates: list[tuple[str, Path]] = [
        # ── Antigravity (Global scope: ~/.gemini/config/skills/)
        # Verified: https://antigravity.google/docs/skills/
        #           https://codelabs.developers.google.com/getting-started-with-antigravity-skills
        ("Antigravity", home / ".gemini" / "config" / "skills"),

        # ── Gemini CLI (Global scope: ~/.gemini/skills/)
        # Verified: https://geminicli.com/docs/cli/tutorials/skills-getting-started/
        ("Gemini CLI", home / ".gemini" / "skills"),

        # ── Claude Code (Global scope: ~/.claude/skills/)
        # Verified: https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code/skills
        ("Claude Code", home / ".claude" / "skills"),

        # ── Cursor (Global scope: ~/.cursor/skills/ + OS User/skills paths)
        # Verified: https://cursor.com/docs/skills
        ("Cursor", home / ".cursor" / "skills"),
        *(
            # Note: APPDATA on Windows already points to ...\AppData\Roaming
            [("Cursor", appdata / "Cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if appdata is not None
            else []
        ),
        *(
            [("Cursor", home / "Library" / "Application Support" / "Cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if sys.platform == "darwin"
            else []
        ),
        *(
            [("Cursor", xdg_config / "cursor" / "User" / "skills")]
            if sys.platform.startswith("linux")
            else []
        ),

        # ── OpenAI Codex CLI (Global scope: ~/.codex/skills/)
        # Verified: https://skills-hub.ai/codex-skills
        ("Codex CLI", home / ".codex" / "skills"),

        # ── OpenCode (Global scope: ~/.config/opencode/skills/)
        # Verified: https://opencode.ai/docs/skills/
        ("OpenCode", xdg_config / "opencode" / "skills"),

        # ── Windsurf / Cascade (Global scope: ~/.codeium/windsurf/skills/)
        # Verified: https://www.ui-skills.com/agents/windsurf
        ("Windsurf (Cascade)", home / ".codeium" / "windsurf" / "skills"),

        # ── Generic .agents standard (Global scope: ~/.agents/skills/)
        # Verified: https://cursor.com/docs/skills & https://opencode.ai/docs/skills/
        ("Generic .agents standard", home / ".agents" / "skills"),
    ]

    # Resolve any symlinks / junctions so we don't double-install
    seen: set[Path] = set()
    result: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []
    for label, path in candidates:
        try:
            resolved = path.resolve()
        except Exception:
            resolved = path
        if resolved in seen:
            continue
        # Only include if the parent directory (or the path itself) already exists on disk
        if path.parent.exists() or path.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(path):
            seen.add(resolved)
            result.append((label, path))

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core installer
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _is_already_up_to_date(dst: Path) -> bool:
    """Return True if `dst` has `.mesh-skill-version` matching `VERSION` and `SKILL.md` exists."""
    if not (dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)):
        return False
    version_file = dst / _VERSION_MARKER_FILE
    skill_md = dst / "SKILL.md"
    if not (version_file.is_file() and skill_md.is_file()):
        return False
    try:
        installed_ver = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        return installed_ver == VERSION
    except OSError:
        return False


def _safe_replace_dir(src: Path, dst: Path, max_retries: int = 5, backoff: float = 0.05) -> None:
    """
    Atomically replace `dst` with `src` on Windows and POSIX.
    Handles transient [WinError 5] Access is denied during rapid directory renames
    caused by Windows file indexers, antivirus locks, or pending handle cleanup (Issue T-001).
    """
    for attempt in range(max_retries):
        try:
            os.replace(src, dst)
            return
        except (PermissionError, OSError):
            if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                raise
            import gc
            import time

            gc.collect()
            time.sleep(backoff * (2**attempt))


def install_skill(
    target_skills_root: Path,
    *,
    overwrite: bool = True,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    force: bool = False,
) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """
    Atomically copy the bundled `mesh-context-layer` skill folder into `target_skills_root`:
      1. If `overwrite=False` and `dst` exists, skip with `"already installed"`.
      2. If `overwrite=True`, `force=False`, and `.mesh-skill-version == VERSION`, skip with `"already up to date"`.
      3. Copy to `dst.with_name(dst.name + ".tmp")`, write `.mesh-skill-version`, then atomically replace `dst`
         (unlinking `dst` first if it is a symlink or Windows junction so `rmtree` never fails).

    Returns `(status_message, was_changed)`.
    """
    src = _skill_source()
    dst = target_skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
    dst_exists = dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)

    if dst_exists and not overwrite and not force:
        return f"already installed at {dst} (skipped)", False

    if dst_exists and not force and _is_already_up_to_date(dst):
        if dry_run:
            # A dry run changed nothing. Reporting True here made callers (and
            # `mesh setup`) claim a write that never happened.
            return f"would install to {dst} (already up to date v{VERSION})", False
        return f"already up to date (v{VERSION}) at {dst}", False

    if dry_run:
        return f"would install to {dst}", True

    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp_dst = dst.with_name(dst.name + ".tmp")
    backup_dst = dst.with_name(dst.name + ".bak")

    if tmp_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(tmp_dst):
        _remove_path_or_link(tmp_dst)
    if backup_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(backup_dst):
        _remove_path_or_link(backup_dst)

    try:
        shutil.copytree(src, tmp_dst)
        (tmp_dst / _VERSION_MARKER_FILE).write_text(f"{VERSION}\n", encoding="utf-8")

        if _is_symlink_or_junction(dst):
            _remove_path_or_link(dst)
            _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
        elif dst.exists():
            _safe_replace_dir(dst, backup_dst)
            try:
                _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
            except Exception:
                if backup_dst.exists() and not dst.exists():
                    _safe_replace_dir(backup_dst, dst)
                raise
            finally:
                if backup_dst.exists():
                    _remove_path_or_link(backup_dst)
        else:
            _safe_replace_dir(tmp_dst, dst)
    finally:
        if tmp_dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(tmp_dst):
            try:
                _remove_path_or_link(tmp_dst)
            except OSError:
                pass

    return f"installed -> {dst}", True


def install_all(
    *,
    assume_yes: bool = False,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    overwrite: bool = True,
    force: bool = False,
    permission_fn: Callable[[str, bool], bool] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Detect all agent skill directories and install the skill into each one.
    Never prompts for permission when `dry_run=True` (Phase 4.10).

    Args:
        assume_yes: Skip interactive y/n prompts.
        dry_run: Preview what would happen without prompting or writing anything.
        overwrite: Overwrite an already-installed copy if outdated.
        force: Reinstall even when `.mesh-skill-version` equals `VERSION`.
        permission_fn: Optional `callable(description, assume_yes=...) -> bool`.
                       Defaults to `daemon.request_user_permission`.

    Returns:
        List of result dicts with keys: `agent`, `path`, `status`, `changed`.
    """
    candidates = _candidate_skill_roots()
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    if not candidates:
        results.append(
            {
                "agent": "—",
                "path": None,
                "status": "No agent skill directories detected on this machine.",
                "changed": False,
            }
        )
        return results

    if permission_fn is None:
        from .daemon import request_user_permission as default_perm_fn

        perm_callable = default_perm_fn
    else:
        perm_callable = permission_fn

    for label, skills_root in candidates:
        dst = skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
        dst_exists = dst.exists() or _is_symlink_or_junction(dst)

        if dst_exists and not overwrite and not force:
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": "already installed (skipped)",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )
            continue

        if not dry_run and dst_exists and not force and _is_already_up_to_date(dst):
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": f"already up to date (v{VERSION})",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )
            continue

        # dry_run=True must never prompt for permission (Phase 4.10)
        if not dry_run:
            action_verb = "update" if dst_exists else "install"
            prompt_desc = (
                f"{action_verb} the Mesh agent skill into {label} skill folder\n"
                f"      ({skills_root / _SKILL_NAME})"
            )
            try:
                approved = perm_callable(prompt_desc, assume_yes=assume_yes)
            except TypeError:
                approved = perm_callable(prompt_desc, assume_yes)

            if not approved:
                results.append(
                    {
                        "agent": label,
                        "path": str(skills_root),
                        "status": "skipped (user declined)",
                        "changed": False,
                    }
                )
                continue

        try:
            msg, changed = install_skill(
                skills_root,
                overwrite=overwrite,
                dry_run=dry_run,
                force=force,
            )
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": msg,
                    "changed": changed,
                }
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append(
                {
                    "agent": label,
                    "path": str(skills_root),
                    "status": f"ERROR: {exc}",
                    "changed": False,
                }
            )

    return results


def get_installed_locations() -> list[Path]:
    """Return all paths where the mesh-context-layer skill is currently installed."""
    installed: list[Path] = []
    for _, skills_root in _candidate_skill_roots():
        dst = skills_root / _SKILL_NAME
        if dst.is_dir() and (dst / "SKILL.md").is_file():
            installed.append(dst)
    return installed

=== FILE: mesh/store.py ===
"""
mesh/store.py — High-performance unified persistence & hybrid search engine for Mesh.

Storage architecture:
  - SQLite (WAL mode) single-source-of-truth per namespace (`~/.mesh/mesh_<namespace>.db`)
  - Strict namespace (`validate_namespace`), memory ID (`validate_memory_id`), and symlink (`reject_symlink`) validation
  - Lowest-layer ingestion guard (`guard_ingest` & `guard_tags`) enforced on every write path
  - First-class provenance (`trust_tier`: 'user' | 'agent' | 'log_captured'), quarantine (`quarantined`),
    and secret locator (`secret_ref`) schema columns with automatic migration
  - Transparent at-rest encryption (`mesh.crypto`) for content, tags, embeddings, queries, and contradictions
  - FTS5 leakage defense: `local_only=True` and encrypted rows are never indexed into on-disk `memories_fts`
    shadow tables; hybrid search uses the in-memory decrypted lexical cache instead
  - Cross-process + in-process re-entrant `namespace_write_lock` (`~/.mesh/.mesh_<ns>.lock`, mode 0600)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import atexit
import contextlib
import json
import logging
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import stat
import sys
import threading
import time
import uuid
from collections import OrderedDict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator, Optional

import numpy as np

from .audit import (
    REDACTED_CONTENT_PLACEHOLDER,
    clear_audit_log,
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir,
    evict_audit_cache,
    get_mesh_dir,
    purge_memory_from_audit,
    reject_symlink,
    scrub_memory_update_from_audit,
    secure_file_permissions,
    validate_namespace,
)
from .crypto import (
    decrypt_blob_if_encrypted,
    decrypt_text_if_encrypted,
    encrypt_blob_for_storage,
    encrypt_text_for_storage,
    is_encrypted_blob,
    is_encrypted_text,
    is_encryption_enabled,
)
from .envutil import env_int
from .guards import guard_ingest, guard_tags
from .secrets import is_credential, normalize_secret_ref, redact_secrets_local

EMBEDDING_DIM = 384

_MAX_POOL_SIZE = DEFAULT_MAX_POOL_SIZE
_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE = DEFAULT_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE

# Pre-mask candidate cut threshold (Phase 2.4)

_REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(
    r"\[REDACTED_(?:SECRET|API_KEY|TOKEN|PASSWORD|JWT|PRIVATE_KEY|content|local_only)[^\]]*\]",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

class NamespaceNotFoundError(ValueError):
    """
    Raised when `MeshStore(namespace, create=False)` or `Mesh(namespace, create=False)`
    is instantiated for a namespace whose SQLite database file does not exist on disk.
    Inherits from `ValueError` for backwards compatibility with existing callers.
    """


class NamespaceLimitExceededError(ValueError):
    """
    Raised when creating a new namespace would exceed the configured namespace cap
    (`MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES`, default 256).
    """


def _normalize_trust_tier(raw_tier: Optional[str], default: str = "user") -> str:
    if not raw_tier:
        return default
    clean = str(raw_tier).strip().lower()
    return clean if clean in VALID_TRUST_TIERS else default


def _parse_secret_ref_json(raw_ref: Any) -> Optional[dict[str, str]]:
    if not raw_ref:
        return None
    try:
        plain = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(raw_ref) if isinstance(raw_ref, str) else raw_ref
        if isinstance(plain, dict):
            return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in plain.items()}
        if isinstance(plain, str) and plain.strip():
            parsed = json.loads(plain)
            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
                return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in parsed.items()}
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _get_max_pool_size() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_POOL_SIZE"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_POOL_SIZE", _MAX_POOL_SIZE, minimum=2)
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES", _MAX_POOL_SIZE, minimum=2)
    return max(2, int(_MAX_POOL_SIZE))


def _get_max_vector_cache_size() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE", _MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE, minimum=2)
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES", _MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE, minimum=2)
    return max(2, int(_MAX_VECTOR_CACHE_SIZE))


def _get_max_namespaces() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_NAMESPACES", _MAX_NAMESPACES, minimum=1)
    return max(1, int(_MAX_NAMESPACES))


def _check_namespace_creation_limit(base_dir: Path, namespace: str) -> None:
    """Enforce a cap on total tracked namespaces before creating a new `.db` file."""
    target_db = (base_dir / f"mesh_{namespace}.db").resolve()
    if target_db.exists():
        return
    max_ns = _get_max_namespaces()
    if not base_dir.exists():
        return
    existing_count = 0
    for p in base_dir.glob("mesh_*.db"):
        if p.is_file() and not p.name.endswith(("-wal", "-shm")):
            existing_count += 1
            if existing_count >= max_ns:
                raise NamespaceLimitExceededError(
                    f"Maximum number of tracked namespaces ({max_ns}) exceeded; "
                    f"refusing to create namespace '{namespace}'."
                )


def _evict_external_namespace_caches(namespace: str) -> None:
    """Evict cached state for `namespace` in audit, write-lock, context_builder, HTTP, MCP, and Dashboard."""
    evict_namespace_write_lock(namespace)
    evict_audit_cache(namespace)

    ctx_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.context_builder")
    if ctx_mod is not None and hasattr(ctx_mod, "evict_context_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            ctx_mod.evict_context_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    http_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.http_server")
    if http_mod is not None and hasattr(http_mod, "evict_http_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            http_mod.evict_http_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    mcp_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.mcp_server")
    if mcp_mod is not None and hasattr(mcp_mod, "evict_mcp_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            mcp_mod.evict_mcp_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass

    dash_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.dashboard")
    if dash_mod is not None and hasattr(dash_mod, "evict_dashboard_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            dash_mod.evict_dashboard_mesh_cache(namespace)
        except Exception:
            pass


def _finalize_evicted_resources(
    evicted: list[tuple[Path, Optional["_PooledConnection"], Optional[str]]]
) -> None:
    """
    Flush telemetry, close pooled connections, and evict upper-layer caches for
    LRU-evicted namespaces AFTER releasing `_CACHE_LOCK`.
    """
    for db_path, conn, ns in evicted:
        try:
            _flush_telemetry_for_db_path(db_path)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if conn is not None:
            conn.close_pooled()
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            _QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS.pop(db_path, None)
            dict.pop(_INITIALIZED_DBS, db_path, None)
            extra_conn = _CONNECTION_POOL.pop(db_path, None)
            if extra_conn is not None:
                extra_conn.close_pooled()
            _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(db_path)
        if ns:
            _evict_external_namespace_caches(ns)


def validate_memory_id(memory_id: str) -> str:
    """
    Validate a memory or contradiction identifier to prevent stored XSS, SQL/HTML injection,
    or control-character corruption.
    Allowed: 1-128 characters of ASCII letters, digits, '.', '_', ':', '-', starting with alphanumeric.
    """
    if not isinstance(memory_id, str):
        raise ValueError("Memory ID must be a string.")
    clean = memory_id.strip()
    if not clean or not _VALID_MEMORY_ID_RE.match(clean):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid memory ID '{memory_id}'. Must match ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{{0,127}}$."
        )
    return clean


# --- Mixed Embedding-Space Guards (Phase 2.1) ---


def _is_mixed_space(row_model: Optional[str], query_model: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    rm = (row_model or "").strip().lower()
    qm = (query_model or "").strip().lower()
    if not rm or not qm:
        return False
    if rm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed") or qm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed"):
        return False
    return rm != qm


def _is_same_known_space(row_model: Optional[str], query_model: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    rm = (row_model or "").strip().lower()
    qm = (query_model or "").strip().lower()
    if not rm or not qm:
        return False
    if rm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed") or qm in ("unknown", "needs-reembed"):
        return False
    return rm == qm


# --- Cross-Process + In-Process Re-Entrant Namespace Write Lock (Phase 2.2) ---


class _NamespaceLockState:
    def __init__(self, namespace: str) -> None:
        self.namespace = namespace
        self.rlock = threading.RLock()
        self.depth = 0
        self.fd: Optional[int] = None
        self.lock_path: Optional[Path] = None


_NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX = threading.Lock()
_NS_LOCK_REGISTRY: dict[tuple[Path, str], _NamespaceLockState] = {}


def _get_ns_lock_path(namespace: str) -> Path:
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    lock_path = base_dir / f".mesh_{clean_ns}.lock"
    reject_symlink(lock_path)
    return lock_path


def _acquire_os_file_lock(lock_path: Path, timeout: float) -> int:
    """
    Acquire an exclusive cross-process advisory file lock on `lock_path`.
    Finding N9: Guarantees `fd` is closed on *any* BaseException (including KeyboardInterrupt)
    before lock acquisition succeeds.
    """
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir(lock_path.parent)
    reject_symlink(lock_path)
    flags = os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT
    if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
        flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
    mode = stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR if os.name != "nt" else 0o600
    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), flags, mode)
    try:
        if os.name != "nt":
            try:
                os.fchmod(fd, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IWUSR)
            except (AttributeError, OSError):
                pass
        else:
            try:
                if os.fstat(fd).st_size == 0:
                    os.write(fd, b"\x00")
            except OSError:
                pass

        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.05, float(timeout))
        sleep_s = 0.002
        while True:
            try:
                if os.name == "nt":
                    import msvcrt

                    os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
                    msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
                else:
                    import fcntl

                    fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                return fd
            except OSError:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise TimeoutError(
                        f"Timed out after {timeout:.1f}s waiting for namespace write lock '{lock_path.name}'."
                    )
                time.sleep(sleep_s)
                sleep_s = min(0.05, sleep_s * 1.4)
    except BaseException:
        try:
            os.close(fd)
        except OSError:
            pass
        raise


def _release_os_file_lock(fd: int) -> None:
    try:
        if os.name == "nt":
            import msvcrt

            try:
                os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
                msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
            except OSError:
                pass
        else:
            import fcntl

            try:
                fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
            except OSError:
                pass
    finally:
        try:
            os.close(fd)
        except OSError:
            pass


@contextlib.contextmanager
def namespace_write_lock(namespace: str, timeout: float = 15.0) -> Iterator[None]:
    """
    Combined per-namespace in-process `threading.RLock` + cross-process advisory file lock
    on `~/.mesh/.mesh_<ns>.lock` (mode 0600 on POSIX).
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    mesh_dir = get_mesh_dir().resolve()
    key = (mesh_dir, clean_ns)
    with _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX:
        state = _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if state is None:
            state = _NamespaceLockState(clean_ns)
            _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY[key] = state

    wait_timeout = max(0.05, float(timeout))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + wait_timeout
    if not state.rlock.acquire(timeout=wait_timeout):
        raise TimeoutError(
            f"Timed out after {timeout:.1f}s waiting for in-process namespace write lock '{clean_ns}'."
        )
    try:
        if state.depth == 0:
            remaining = max(0.05, deadline - time.monotonic())
            lock_path = _get_ns_lock_path(clean_ns)
            state.fd = _acquire_os_file_lock(lock_path, timeout=remaining)
            state.lock_path = lock_path
        state.depth += 1
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            state.depth -= 1
            if state.depth == 0 and state.fd is not None:
                fd_to_close = state.fd
                state.fd = None
                _release_os_file_lock(fd_to_close)
    finally:
        state.rlock.release()


def evict_namespace_write_lock(namespace: str) -> None:
    """Evict the in-process lock registry entry for a namespace."""
    try:
        clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    except ValueError:
        return
    mesh_dir = get_mesh_dir().resolve()
    key = (mesh_dir, clean_ns)
    with _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY_MUTEX:
        state = _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if state is None:
            return
        acquired = state.rlock.acquire(timeout=0.05)
        if not acquired:
            return
        try:
            if state.depth > 0:
                return
            _NS_LOCK_REGISTRY.pop(key, None)
            if state.fd is not None:
                _release_os_file_lock(state.fd)
                state.fd = None
        finally:
            state.rlock.release()


def _clean_possessives(text: str) -> str:
    s = text.lower().replace("\u2019", "'").replace("\u2018", "'").replace("`", "'")
    return re.sub(r"'s\b", "", s)


def _fast_stem(w: str) -> str:
    if len(w) <= 3:
        return w
    if w.endswith("ies") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-3] + "y"
    if w.endswith("ing") and len(w) > 5:
        base = w[:-3]
        return base[:-1] if len(base) >= 3 and base[-1] == base[-2] else base
    if w.endswith("ed") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("es") and len(w) > 4:
        return w[:-2]
    if w.endswith("s") and not w.endswith("ss") and len(w) > 3:
        return w[:-1]
    return w


# --- Bidirectional Tight Domain Synonym & Compound Expansion Engine (Phase 2.5) ---
_SYNONYM_GROUPS: list[set[str]] = [
    {"color", "colour", "hue", "shade", "tint"},
    {"blue", "navy", "azure", "cyan", "cobalt", "teal", "sapphire", "indigo"},
    {"red", "crimson", "scarlet", "maroon", "ruby", "vermilion", "burgundy"},
    {"green", "emerald", "lime", "olive", "jade", "mint"},
    {"yellow", "gold", "golden", "amber", "lemon", "mustard"},
    {"purple", "violet", "lavender", "magenta", "plum", "lilac"},
    {"dark", "night", "black", "dim", "obsidian", "charcoal"},
    {"light", "bright", "day", "white", "ivory", "pale"},
    {"pen", "pens", "biro", "ballpoint", "marker", "stylus", "highlighter", "stationery", "pencil"},
    {"anime", "manga", "animation", "otaku", "cartoon"},
    {"favorite", "favourite", "preferred", "beloved", "likes", "loves"},
    {"laptop", "notebook", "macbook", "computer", "pc", "workstation", "machine"},
    {"phone", "smartphone", "mobile", "cellphone", "iphone", "android", "handset"},
    {"db", "database", "databases", "datastore"},
    {"postgres", "postgresql", "psql", "pg", "pgbouncer"},
    {"redis", "valkey", "memcached", "inmemory"},
    {"sqlite", "sqlite3"},
    {"mongo", "mongodb", "documentdb"},
    {"auth", "authentication", "authorization", "login", "signin", "sso", "oauth", "oidc", "rbac"},
    {"jwt", "token", "tokens", "bearer", "session", "cookie", "cookies"},
    {"password", "passphrase", "secret", "secrets", "credential", "credentials", "apikey", "vault"},
    {"prod", "production"},
    {"stage", "staging", "preprod", "uat"},
    {"dev", "development", "localhost", "loopback"},
    {"deploy", "deployment", "rollout"},
    {"url", "uri", "link", "hyperlink"},
    {"ip", "address", "ipv4", "ipv6"},
    {"host", "hostname"},
    {"k8s", "kubernetes", "kubectl"},
    {"bug", "bugs", "error", "errors", "exception", "crash", "failure", "fault", "defect", "incident"},
    {"fix", "fixed", "patch", "resolve", "resolved", "workaround", "remedy", "repair"},
    {"config", "configuration", "settings", "env", "environment"},
    {"limit", "limits", "ratelimit", "quota", "throttle", "throttling"},
    {"fast", "latency", "performance", "throughput", "bottleneck", "benchmark"},
    {"test", "tests", "testing", "pytest", "unittest"},
]

_COMPOUND_SPLITS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "onepiece": ("one", "piece"),
    "postgresql": ("postgres", "sql"),
    "pgbouncer": ("pg", "bouncer"),
    "typescript": ("type", "script"),
    "javascript": ("java", "script"),
    "apikey": ("api", "key"),
    "localstorage": ("local", "storage"),
    "httponly": ("http", "only"),
    "ratelimit": ("rate", "limit"),
    "darkmode": ("dark", "mode"),
    "lightmode": ("light", "mode"),
    "codebase": ("code", "base"),
    "healthcheck": ("health", "check"),
}

_SYNONYM_MAP: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
for _grp in _SYNONYM_GROUPS:
    _normalized_grp = {t.lower() for t in _grp}
    _stemmed_grp = {_fast_stem(t) for t in _normalized_grp}
    _all_forms = _normalized_grp | _stemmed_grp
    for _tok in _all_forms:
        _SYNONYM_MAP.setdefault(_tok, set()).update(_all_forms - {_tok})

_SPLIT_TO_COMPOUND: dict[tuple[str, ...], str] = {v: k for k, v in _COMPOUND_SPLITS.items()}


def _extract_search_tokens(text: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], set[str]]:
    norm = _clean_possessives(text)
    raw_tokens = [t.strip("._:-/") for t in _TOKEN_RE.findall(norm)]
    raw_tokens = [t for t in raw_tokens if len(t) >= 2]
    word_tokens = _WORD_RE.findall(norm)

    compact_ident_tokens: list[str] = []
    for rt in raw_tokens:
        if any(ch in rt for ch in ".-_:/"):
            compact = _PUNCT_SPLIT_RE.sub("", rt)
            if len(compact) >= 3 and compact != rt:
                compact_ident_tokens.append(compact)

    combined = list(dict.fromkeys([*raw_tokens, *compact_ident_tokens, *word_tokens]))
    content_tokens = [t for t in combined if t not in _LEXICAL_STOPWORDS and len(t) >= 2]
    if not content_tokens:
        content_tokens = [t for t in combined if len(t) >= 2]
    stemmed_set = {_fast_stem(t) for t in content_tokens} | set(content_tokens)
    return combined, content_tokens, stemmed_set


def expand_query_tokens(content_tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
    expansions: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    lower_tokens = [t.lower() for t in content_tokens]

    adjacent_compounds: dict[int, str] = {}
    for i in range(len(lower_tokens) - 1):
        pair = (lower_tokens[i], lower_tokens[i + 1])
        if pair in _SPLIT_TO_COMPOUND:
            comp = _SPLIT_TO_COMPOUND[pair]
            adjacent_compounds[i] = comp
            adjacent_compounds[i + 1] = comp

    for idx, tok in enumerate(lower_tokens):
        stem = _fast_stem(tok)
        syns: set[str] = set()
        if tok in _SYNONYM_MAP:
            syns.update(_SYNONYM_MAP[tok])
        if stem in _SYNONYM_MAP:
            syns.update(_SYNONYM_MAP[stem])
        if tok in _COMPOUND_SPLITS:
            syns.update(_COMPOUND_SPLITS[tok])

        if any(c in tok for c in ".-_:/"):
            compact = _PUNCT_SPLIT_RE.sub("", tok)
            if len(compact) >= 3:
                syns.add(compact)
                if compact in _COMPOUND_SPLITS:
                    syns.update(_COMPOUND_SPLITS[compact])
                if compact in _SYNONYM_MAP:
                    syns.update(_SYNONYM_MAP[compact])
            parts = [p for p in _PUNCT_SPLIT_RE.split(tok) if len(p) >= 2]
            syns.update(parts)

        if idx in adjacent_compounds:
            syns.add(adjacent_compounds[idx])

        syns.discard(tok)
        syns.discard(stem)
        expansions[tok] = syns
    return expansions


def _make_snippet(content: str, max_len: int = 110) -> str:
    clean = " ".join(str(content or "").strip().split())
    if len(clean) <= max_len:
        return clean
    return clean[: max_len - 3].rstrip() + "..."


def _build_row_lexical_entry(content: str, tags_list: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    doc_norm = _clean_possessives(content)
    doc_compact = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", doc_norm)
    _, doc_tokens, doc_stems = _extract_search_tokens(content)
    tag_tokens = {str(t).strip().lower() for t in tags_list if str(t).strip()}
    tag_stems = {_fast_stem(t) for t in tag_tokens}
    tag_compacts = {re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", t) for t in tag_tokens if len(t) >= 2}
    doc_compacts = {re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", t) for t in doc_tokens if len(t) >= 2}

    all_doc_tokens = set(doc_tokens) | tag_tokens | doc_compacts | tag_compacts
    all_doc_stems = doc_stems | tag_stems | {_fast_stem(t) for t in doc_compacts}
    combined_df_stems = all_doc_stems | set(doc_tokens)
    return {
        "doc_norm": doc_norm,
        "doc_compact": doc_compact,
        "doc_len": max(1, len(doc_tokens)),
        "tag_tokens": tag_tokens,
        "tag_stems": tag_stems,
        "tag_compacts": tag_compacts,
        "all_doc_tokens": all_doc_tokens,
        "all_doc_stems": all_doc_stems,
        "df_stems": combined_df_stems,
    }


def _row_needs_healing(row_meta: dict[str, Any], vec: Optional[np.ndarray]) -> bool:
    emb_model = row_meta.get("embedding_model")
    if emb_model in ("hash-384", "needs-reembed", "unknown", None):
        return True
    if vec is None or vec.size != EMBEDDING_DIM:
        return True
    if np.all(vec[368:] == 0.0) and int(np.count_nonzero(vec == 0.0)) >= 16:
        return True
    return False


class _PooledConnection(sqlite3.Connection):
    """A pooled SQLite connection serialized across threads.

    `check_same_thread=False` disables sqlite3's thread check but does not make
    the connection safe to share: the handle, statement state and row factory
    are all mutable shared state. A per-connection RLock therefore serializes
    every ``with store._connect()`` block.

    Checkouts are reference-counted so LRU eviction can *retire* a connection
    without closing it while another thread is still using it. The physical
    close happens when the last user checks in.
    """

    def __init__(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self._mesh_lock = threading.RLock()
        self._mesh_state_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._mesh_users = 0
        self._mesh_retired = False
        self._mesh_closed = False

    def __enter__(self) -> "_PooledConnection":
        self._mesh_lock.acquire()
        try:
            with self._mesh_state_lock:
                closed = self._mesh_closed
            if closed:
                raise sqlite3.ProgrammingError("Cannot operate on a closed database.")
            super().__enter__()
            return self
        except BaseException:
            self._mesh_lock.release()
            raise

    def __exit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc_val: Any, exc_tb: Any) -> bool:
        try:
            return super().__exit__(exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb)
        finally:
            self._mesh_lock.release()

    def close(self) -> None:
        # Pooled connections are closed through the checkout/retire lifecycle.
        pass

    def checkout(self) -> None:
        """Reserve this connection for one caller.

        Must be called while ``_CACHE_LOCK`` is held so LRU eviction cannot
        retire the connection between lookup and checkout.
        """
        with self._mesh_state_lock:
            if self._mesh_closed or self._mesh_retired:
                raise sqlite3.ProgrammingError("Cannot operate on a closed database.")
            self._mesh_users += 1

    def checkin(self) -> None:
        should_close = False
        with self._mesh_state_lock:
            if self._mesh_users > 0:
                self._mesh_users -= 1
            if self._mesh_retired and self._mesh_users == 0 and not self._mesh_closed:
                self._mesh_closed = True
                should_close = True
        if should_close:
            self._close_physical()

    def retire(self) -> None:
        """Mark for closure; close immediately only when nobody is using it.

        Never waits for an in-flight user: eviction must not block on a thread
        that is still inside ``with store._connect()``.
        """
        should_close = False
        with self._mesh_state_lock:
            self._mesh_retired = True
            if self._mesh_users == 0 and not self._mesh_closed:
                self._mesh_closed = True
                should_close = True
        if should_close:
            self._close_physical()

    def close_pooled(self) -> None:
        self.retire()

    def _close_physical(self) -> None:
        try:
            super().close()
        except Exception:
            pass


class _ConnectionCheckout:
    """Context manager returned by ``MeshStore._connect()``.

    The reference is taken inside ``_connect()`` while ``_CACHE_LOCK`` is held,
    so an evicting thread cannot close the connection between checkout and
    ``__enter__``.
    """

    __slots__ = ("_conn", "_checked_in")

    def __init__(self, conn: _PooledConnection) -> None:
        self._conn = conn
        self._checked_in = False

    def __enter__(self) -> _PooledConnection:
        try:
            return self._conn.__enter__()
        except BaseException:
            self._checkin()
            raise

    def __exit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc_val: Any, exc_tb: Any) -> bool:
        try:
            return self._conn.__exit__(exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb)
        finally:
            self._checkin()

    def _checkin(self) -> None:
        if not self._checked_in:
            self._checked_in = True
            self._conn.checkin()


def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
    return _utc_now().isoformat()


def _parse_iso_utc(ts: Optional[str]) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if not ts or not isinstance(ts, str):
        return None
    try:
        cleaned = ts.strip()
        if not cleaned:
            return None
        if cleaned.endswith(("Z", "z")):
            cleaned = cleaned[:-1] + "+00:00"
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(cleaned)
        if dt.tzinfo is None:
            return dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    except Exception:
        return None


def _compute_row_effective_confidence(
    row: dict[str, Any],
    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
) -> tuple[float, bool]:
    stored_conf = float(row.get("confidence", 1.0))
    ttl_raw = row.get("ttl_days")
    if ttl_raw is None:
        return round(stored_conf, 4), stored_conf < STALE_THRESHOLD
    try:
        ttl = float(ttl_raw)
        if ttl <= 0.0:
            return round(stored_conf, 4), stored_conf < STALE_THRESHOLD
        decay_start = row.get("decay_start_at") or row.get("created_at")
        start_dt = _parse_iso_utc(decay_start)
        if start_dt is None:
            return round(stored_conf, 4), stored_conf < STALE_THRESHOLD
        ref_now = now if now is not None else _utc_now()
        days_elapsed = max(0.0, (ref_now - start_dt).total_seconds() / 86400.0)
        decay_factor = max(0.0, min(1.0, 1.0 - (days_elapsed / ttl)))
        effective_conf = round(stored_conf * decay_factor, 4)
        return effective_conf, effective_conf < STALE_THRESHOLD
    except Exception:
        return round(stored_conf, 4), stored_conf < STALE_THRESHOLD


def _get_db_stamp(db_path: Path) -> Optional[DbStamp]:
    if not db_path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        st = db_path.stat()
        wal_path = Path(f"{db_path}-wal")
        if wal_path.exists():
            try:
                wst = wal_path.stat()
                return (st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_size, wst.st_mtime_ns, wst.st_size)
            except OSError:
                pass
        return (st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_size, 0, 0)
    except OSError:
        return None


def _normalize_vector(vec: list[float] | np.ndarray, enforce_dim: bool = True) -> np.ndarray:
    arr = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)
    if enforce_dim and arr.size != EMBEDDING_DIM:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid embedding dimension {arr.size}; Mesh requires {EMBEDDING_DIM}-dimensional vectors."
        )
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(arr))
    if norm > 1e-12:
        arr = arr / norm
    return np.ascontiguousarray(arr, dtype=np.float32)


def _vector_to_blob(vec: list[float] | np.ndarray) -> bytes:
    """Serialize a vector into a normalized float32 byte buffer (encrypting if at-rest encryption is active)."""
    raw = _normalize_vector(vec).tobytes()
    if is_encryption_enabled():
        return encrypt_blob_for_storage(raw) or raw
    return raw


def _blob_to_vector(blob: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    """Deserialize a float32 byte buffer (decrypting first if it carries a `MESHB1` header)."""
    if is_encrypted_blob(blob):
        plain = decrypt_blob_if_encrypted(blob)
        if plain is None:
            return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float32)
        return np.frombuffer(plain, dtype=np.float32)
    return np.frombuffer(blob, dtype=np.float32)


def _parse_tags_json(raw_tags: Any) -> list[str]:
    if not raw_tags:
        return []
    if isinstance(raw_tags, list):
        return [str(t).strip() for t in raw_tags if str(t).strip()]
    if isinstance(raw_tags, str):
        s = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(raw_tags).strip()
        if not s or s == "[]":
            return []
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(s)
            if isinstance(parsed, list):
                return [str(t).strip() for t in parsed if str(t).strip()]
        except Exception:
            pass
    return []


def _enforce_store_guard(
    content: str,
    tags_list: list[str],
    local_only: bool,
    allow_redacted_public: bool = False,
) -> tuple[str, list[str], bool]:
    """
    Finding S11: Enforce `guard_ingest` and `guard_tags` at the `MeshStore` boundary
    so no caller (`store.upsert`, `store.upsert_batch`, `store.update`, `collection.upsert`)
    can bypass prompt-injection rejection or secret redaction.
    """
    g_res = guard_ingest(content, local_only=local_only)
    if g_res.rejected:
        raise ValueError(g_res.reason or "Memory content rejected by ingestion guard.")
    clean_tags, tags_private, _ = guard_tags(tags_list, local_only=g_res.local_only)
    final_local = bool(local_only or g_res.local_only or tags_private)
    if not final_local and not allow_redacted_public and _REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER_RE.search(g_res.content):
        final_local = True
    return g_res.content, clean_tags, final_local


_CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT: int = 0


def get_cache_rebuild_count() -> int:
    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        return _CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT


def reset_cache_rebuild_count() -> None:
    global _CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT
    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        _CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT = 0
        _VectorCacheEntry.build_count = 0


class _VectorCacheEntry:
    build_count: int = 0

    __slots__ = (
        "stamp",
        "data_version",
        "content_version",
        "mtime_ns",
        "file_size",
        "ids",
        "id_to_idx",
        "_matrix_buf",
        "dim",
        "all_ids",
        "rows_by_id",
        "lexical_by_id",
        "stem_df",
        "stem_to_ids",
        "needs_healing_ids",
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        stamp: DbStamp,
        ids: list[str],
        matrix: Optional[np.ndarray],
        all_ids: Optional[set[str]] = None,
        rows_by_id: Optional[dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        data_version: int = 0,
        content_version: Optional[int] = None,
        needs_healing_ids: Optional[set[str]] = None,
        reused_lexical: Optional[dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = None,
    ):
        global _CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT
        self.stamp: DbStamp = stamp
        self.data_version: int = data_version
        self.content_version: Optional[int] = content_version
        self.mtime_ns: int = stamp[0]
        self.file_size: int = stamp[1]
        self.ids: list[str] = ids
        self.id_to_idx: dict[str, int] = {mid: idx for idx, mid in enumerate(ids)}
        self.all_ids: set[str] = all_ids if all_ids is not None else set(ids)
        self.rows_by_id: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = rows_by_id if rows_by_id is not None else {}
        self.lexical_by_id: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self.stem_df: dict[str, int] = {}
        self.stem_to_ids: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
        self.needs_healing_ids: set[str] = (
            set(needs_healing_ids) if needs_healing_ids is not None else set()
        )

        if matrix is not None and matrix.ndim == 2 and matrix.shape[0] > 0:
            n_rows, dim = int(matrix.shape[0]), int(matrix.shape[1])
            cap = max(64, 1 << (n_rows - 1).bit_length())
            buf = np.empty((cap, dim), dtype=np.float32)
            buf[:n_rows] = matrix
            self._matrix_buf: Optional[np.ndarray] = buf
            self.dim: int = dim
        else:
            self._matrix_buf = None
            self.dim = 0

        for mid, row_meta in self.rows_by_id.items():
            if reused_lexical and mid in reused_lexical:
                lex = reused_lexical[mid]
                self.lexical_by_id[mid] = lex
                for s in lex["df_stems"]:
                    self.stem_df[s] = self.stem_df.get(s, 0) + 1
                    self.stem_to_ids.setdefault(s, set()).add(mid)
            else:
                self._index_row_lexical(mid, row_meta)

            if needs_healing_ids is None:
                idx = self.id_to_idx.get(mid)
                vec = self._matrix_buf[idx] if (idx is not None and self._matrix_buf is not None) else None
                if _row_needs_healing(row_meta, vec):
                    self.needs_healing_ids.add(mid)

        _VectorCacheEntry.build_count += 1
        _CACHE_REBUILD_COUNT += 1

    def _index_row_lexical(self, memory_id: str, row_meta: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        old_lex = self.lexical_by_id.pop(memory_id, None)
        if old_lex is not None:
            for s in old_lex["df_stems"]:
                new_cnt = self.stem_df.get(s, 1) - 1
                if new_cnt <= 0:
                    self.stem_df.pop(s, None)
                else:
                    self.stem_df[s] = new_cnt
                s_set = self.stem_to_ids.get(s)
                if s_set is not None:
                    s_set.discard(memory_id)
                    if not s_set:
                        self.stem_to_ids.pop(s, None)

        content = str(row_meta.get("content", ""))
        tags_list = row_meta.get("tags_list") or _parse_tags_json(row_meta.get("tags"))
        new_lex = _build_row_lexical_entry(content, tags_list)
        self.lexical_by_id[memory_id] = new_lex
        for s in new_lex["df_stems"]:
            self.stem_df[s] = self.stem_df.get(s, 0) + 1
            self.stem_to_ids.setdefault(s, set()).add(memory_id)

    def _unindex_row_lexical(self, memory_id: str) -> None:
        old_lex = self.lexical_by_id.pop(memory_id, None)
        if old_lex is not None:
            for s in old_lex["df_stems"]:
                new_cnt = self.stem_df.get(s, 1) - 1
                if new_cnt <= 0:
                    self.stem_df.pop(s, None)
                else:
                    self.stem_df[s] = new_cnt
                s_set = self.stem_to_ids.get(s)
                if s_set is not None:
                    s_set.discard(memory_id)
                    if not s_set:
                        self.stem_to_ids.pop(s, None)

    @property
    def matrix(self) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
        n = len(self.ids)
        if self._matrix_buf is None or n == 0:
            return None
        return self._matrix_buf[:n]

    def snapshot(
        self,
        copy_matrix: bool = True,
    ) -> tuple[list[str], Optional[np.ndarray], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            ids_copy = list(self.ids)
            if self._matrix_buf is not None and ids_copy:
                mat_slice = self._matrix_buf[: len(ids_copy)]
                mat_view = mat_slice.copy() if copy_matrix else mat_slice
            else:
                mat_view = None
            rows_copy = dict(self.rows_by_id)
            return ids_copy, mat_view, rows_copy

    def similarity_snapshot(
        self,
        query_vec: np.ndarray,
    ) -> tuple[list[str], Optional[np.ndarray], dict[str, dict[str, Any]]]:
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            ids_copy = list(self.ids)
            if self._matrix_buf is None or not ids_copy:
                return ids_copy, None, dict(self.rows_by_id)
            mat_slice = self._matrix_buf[: len(ids_copy)]
            if query_vec.ndim != 1 or query_vec.shape[0] != mat_slice.shape[1]:
                return ids_copy, None, dict(self.rows_by_id)
            return ids_copy, mat_slice @ query_vec, dict(self.rows_by_id)

    def update_stamp(
        self,
        stamp: DbStamp,
        data_version: Optional[int] = None,
        content_version: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.stamp = stamp
        self.mtime_ns = stamp[0]
        self.file_size = stamp[1]
        if data_version is not None:
            self.data_version = data_version
        if content_version is not None:
            self.content_version = content_version

    def upsert_vector(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
        vec: Optional[np.ndarray],
        row_meta: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        self.all_ids.add(memory_id)
        if row_meta is not None:
            self.rows_by_id[memory_id] = dict(row_meta)
            self._index_row_lexical(memory_id, row_meta)
            if _row_needs_healing(row_meta, vec):
                self.needs_healing_ids.add(memory_id)
            else:
                self.needs_healing_ids.discard(memory_id)

        if vec is None or vec.size == 0:
            return True

        vec_1d = np.ascontiguousarray(vec.reshape(-1), dtype=np.float32)
        if vec_1d.shape[0] != EMBEDDING_DIM:
            return False

        existing_idx = self.id_to_idx.get(memory_id)
        if existing_idx is not None:
            if self._matrix_buf is None or vec_1d.shape[0] != self.dim:
                return False
            self._matrix_buf[existing_idx] = vec_1d
            return True

        n = len(self.ids)
        if self._matrix_buf is None or n == 0:
            dim = int(vec_1d.shape[0])
            buf = np.empty((64, dim), dtype=np.float32)
            buf[0] = vec_1d
            self._matrix_buf = buf
            self.dim = dim
            self.ids = [memory_id]
            self.id_to_idx = {memory_id: 0}
            return True

        if vec_1d.shape[0] != self.dim:
            return False

        if n >= self._matrix_buf.shape[0]:
            new_cap = max(64, self._matrix_buf.shape[0] * 2)
            new_buf = np.empty((new_cap, self.dim), dtype=np.float32)
            new_buf[:n] = self._matrix_buf[:n]
            self._matrix_buf = new_buf

        self._matrix_buf[n] = vec_1d
        self.id_to_idx[memory_id] = n
        self.ids.append(memory_id)
        return True

    def delete_vector(self, memory_id: str) -> None:
        self.all_ids.discard(memory_id)
        self.needs_healing_ids.discard(memory_id)
        self.rows_by_id.pop(memory_id, None)
        self._unindex_row_lexical(memory_id)
        idx = self.id_to_idx.pop(memory_id, None)
        if idx is None:
            return
        last_idx = len(self.ids) - 1
        if idx != last_idx:
            last_id = self.ids[last_idx]
            self.ids[idx] = last_id
            self.id_to_idx[last_id] = idx
            if self._matrix_buf is not None:
                self._matrix_buf[idx] = self._matrix_buf[last_idx]
        self.ids.pop()
        if not self.ids or self._matrix_buf is None:
            self._matrix_buf = None
            self.dim = 0


_CACHE_LOCK = threading.RLock()
_CONNECTION_POOL: OrderedDict[Path, _PooledConnection] = OrderedDict()
_DB_PATH_NAMESPACES: dict[Path, str] = {}
_VECTOR_CACHE: OrderedDict[tuple[Path, str], _VectorCacheEntry] = OrderedDict()


def _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(db_path: Path) -> None:
    for key in [k for k in _VECTOR_CACHE if k[0] == db_path]:
        _VECTOR_CACHE.pop(key, None)


_QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS: dict[Path, int] = {}
_TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS: dict[tuple[str, str], np.ndarray] = {}


# --- Batched Telemetry Buffer (Phase 2.3B) ---


class _TelemetryBuffer:
    def __init__(self, db_path: Path, namespace: str) -> None:
        self.db_path = db_path
        self.namespace = namespace
        self.access_increments: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {}
        self.query_rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, Optional[float], int, str, int]] = []
        self.event_count: int = 0
        self.first_event_ts: float = 0.0


_TELEMETRY_LOCK = threading.RLock()
_TELEMETRY_BUFFERS: dict[Path, _TelemetryBuffer] = {}


def _get_telemetry_batch_limit() -> int:
    raw = os.environ.get("MESH_TELEMETRY_BATCH", "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return 50
    try:
        return max(0, int(raw))
    except ValueError:
        return 50


def _get_telemetry_flush_seconds() -> float:
    raw = os.environ.get("MESH_TELEMETRY_FLUSH_SECONDS", "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return 5.0
    try:
        return max(0.0, float(raw))
    except ValueError:
        return 5.0


def flush_all_telemetry() -> None:
    """Flush all pending buffered recall telemetry across all namespaces."""
    with _TELEMETRY_LOCK:
        paths = list(_TELEMETRY_BUFFERS.keys())
    for p in paths:
        try:
            _flush_telemetry_for_db_path(p)
        except Exception:
            continue


def _requeue_telemetry(
    resolved: Path,
    namespace: str,
    inc_items: list[tuple[str, tuple[int, str]]],
    q_rows: list[tuple],
) -> None:
    with _TELEMETRY_LOCK:
        buf = _TELEMETRY_BUFFERS.get(resolved)
        if buf is None:
            buf = _TelemetryBuffer(db_path=resolved, namespace=namespace)
            _TELEMETRY_BUFFERS[resolved] = buf
        for mid, (delta, accessed_at) in inc_items:
            prev = buf.access_increments.get(mid)
            if prev is None:
                buf.access_increments[mid] = (delta, accessed_at)
            else:
                buf.access_increments[mid] = (
                    prev[0] + delta,
                    max(prev[1], accessed_at),
                )
        if q_rows:
            buf.query_rows.extend(q_rows)
        buf.event_count += len(inc_items) + len(q_rows)
        if buf.event_count == len(inc_items) + len(q_rows):
            buf.first_event_ts = time.monotonic()


def _flush_telemetry_for_db_path(db_path: Path) -> None:
    resolved = db_path.resolve()
    with _TELEMETRY_LOCK:
        buf = _TELEMETRY_BUFFERS.pop(resolved, None)
        if buf is None or (not buf.access_increments and not buf.query_rows):
            return
        inc_items = list(buf.access_increments.items())
        q_rows = list(buf.query_rows)
        ns = buf.namespace

    if not resolved.exists():
        _requeue_telemetry(resolved, ns, inc_items, q_rows)
        return

    try:
        enc_on = is_encryption_enabled()
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            store = MeshStore(namespace=ns, create=False)
            q_writes = _QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS.get(resolved, 0) + len(q_rows)
            _QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS[resolved] = q_writes
            with store._connect() as conn:
                conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
                current_cv: Optional[int] = None
                try:
                    for mid, (delta, accessed_at) in inc_items:
                        conn.execute(
                            """
                            UPDATE memories
                            SET access_count = access_count + ?,
                                accessed_at = ?
                            WHERE namespace = ? AND id = ?
                            """,
                            (delta, accessed_at, ns, mid),
                        )
                    if q_rows:
                        rows_to_insert = [
                            (
                                qid,
                                q_ns,
                                encrypt_text_for_storage(q_text) if enc_on else q_text,
                                aid,
                                b_sim,
                                r_cnt,
                                ts,
                                p_flag,
                            )
                            for (qid, q_ns, q_text, aid, b_sim, r_cnt, ts, p_flag) in q_rows
                        ]
                        conn.executemany(
                            """
                            INSERT INTO queries
                            (id, namespace, query_text, agent_id, best_similarity, result_count, timestamp, private_query)
                            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                            """,
                            rows_to_insert,
                        )
                        if q_writes >= 100:
                            _QUERY_WRITE_COUNTS[resolved] = 0
