                "memory_type": row.get("memory_type", "fact"),
                "confidence": float(row.get("confidence", 1.0)),
                "effective_confidence": eff_conf,
                "is_stale": is_stale,
                "tags": _parse_tags_json(dec_tags),
                "source_agent": row.get("source_agent", "unknown"),
                "created_at": row.get("created_at", ""),
                "accessed_at": row.get("accessed_at", ""),
                "access_count": int(row.get("access_count") or 0),
                "ttl_days": row.get("ttl_days"),
            }
            whitelisted = {k: candidate[k] for k in _PATTERNS_MEMORY_WHITELIST}

            if is_stale:
                stale_memories.append(whitelisted)

            if whitelisted["access_count"] == 0:
                created_dt = _parse_iso_utc(whitelisted["created_at"])
                if created_dt is not None and created_dt <= seven_days_ago:
                    unused_memories.append(whitelisted)

        unused_memories.sort(key=lambda r: str(r.get("created_at") or ""))
        stale_memories.sort(key=lambda r: float(r.get("effective_confidence", 0.0)))

        return {
            "top_topics": top_topics,
            "memory_gaps": memory_gaps,
            "unused_memories": unused_memories[:25],
            "stale_memories": stale_memories[:25],
        }

    # =========================================================================
    # Work Items & Work Events (Goals, Tasks, Project Brief, Changelog)
    # =========================================================================

    def _resolve_work_item_locked(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        clean_id = (item_id or "").strip()
        if not clean_id:
            return None
        # 1. Exact UUID
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND id = ?",
            (self.namespace, clean_id),
        ).fetchone()
        if row:
            return dict(row)
        # 2. Exact short_id (e.g. task-1, goal-2)
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND lower(short_id) = lower(?)",
            (self.namespace, clean_id),
        ).fetchone()
        if row:
            return dict(row)
        # 3. Hex prefix match on id (at least 4 chars)
        if len(clean_id) >= 4:
            rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND id LIKE ? || '%'",
                (self.namespace, clean_id),
            ).fetchall()
            if len(rows) == 1:
                return dict(rows[0])
        return None

    def _not_found_error_message(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_id: str) -> str:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT short_id, title FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 5",
            (self.namespace,),
        ).fetchall()
        if not rows:
            return (
                f"Work item '{item_id}' not found in namespace '{self.namespace}'. "
                "No work items exist yet in this namespace; use mesh_task_create to create one first."
            )
        available = [f"{r[0]} ({r[1][:30]})" for r in rows]
        return (
            f"Work item '{item_id}' not found in namespace '{self.namespace}'. "
            f"Available work items: {', '.join(available)}."
        )

    def _generate_short_id_locked(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str) -> str:
        count_row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND kind = ?",
            (self.namespace, kind),
        ).fetchone()
        seq = int(count_row[0] or 0) + 1
        short_id = f"{kind}-{seq}"
        if conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND short_id = ?",
            (self.namespace, short_id),
        ).fetchone():
            short_id = f"{kind}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}"
        return short_id

    def create_work_item(
        self,
        title: str,
        kind: str = "task",
        parent_id: Optional[str] = None,
        description: str = "",
        priority: str = "medium",
        tags: Optional[list[str]] = None,
        created_by: str = "agent",
        local_only: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        clean_kind = str(kind or "task").strip().lower()
        if clean_kind not in ("goal", "task"):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid kind '{kind}'. Must be 'goal' or 'task'.")
        clean_priority = str(priority or "medium").strip().lower()
        if clean_priority not in ("low", "medium", "high", "critical"):
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid priority '{priority}'. Must be 'low', 'medium', 'high', or 'critical'.")

        guard_t = guard_ingest(title or "", local_only=local_only)
        if guard_t.rejected:
            raise ValueError(f"Work item title rejected: {guard_t.reason}")
        clean_title = guard_t.content.strip()
        if not clean_title:
            raise ValueError("Work item title cannot be empty.")

        clean_desc = ""
        is_priv = bool(guard_t.local_only)
        if description and str(description).strip():
            guard_d = guard_ingest(description, local_only=guard_t.local_only)
            if guard_d.rejected:
                raise ValueError(f"Work item description rejected: {guard_d.reason}")
            clean_desc = guard_d.content.strip()
            is_priv = bool(is_priv or guard_d.local_only)

        clean_tags, tags_priv, _ = guard_tags(tags or [], local_only=is_priv)
        is_priv = bool(is_priv or tags_priv)

        item_uuid = uuid.uuid4().hex
        now_iso = _utc_now_iso()

        with namespace_write_lock(self.namespace):
            with self._connect() as conn:
                conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
                resolved_parent: Optional[str] = None
                if parent_id:
                    parent_row = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, parent_id)
                    if not parent_row:
                        raise ValueError(f"Parent {self._not_found_error_message(conn, parent_id)}")
                    resolved_parent = parent_row["id"]

                count_row = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND kind = ?",
                    (self.namespace, clean_kind),
                ).fetchone()
                seq = int(count_row[0] or 0) + 1
                short_id = f"{clean_kind}-{seq}"
                if conn.execute("SELECT 1 FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ? AND short_id = ?", (self.namespace, short_id)).fetchone():
                    short_id = f"{clean_kind}-{item_uuid[:6]}"

                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO work_items
                    (id, short_id, namespace, kind, parent_id, title, description, status, priority,
                     created_by, created_at, started_at, completed_at, completed_by, summary, evidence, tags, local_only)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'todo', ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, '[]', ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        item_uuid,
                        short_id,
                        self.namespace,
                        clean_kind,
                        resolved_parent,
                        clean_title,
                        clean_desc,
                        clean_priority,
                        str(created_by or "agent"),
                        now_iso,
                        json.dumps(clean_tags),
                        1 if is_priv else 0,
                    ),
                )
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO work_events
                    (item_id, namespace, event_type, old_status, new_status, note, agent_id, timestamp)
                    VALUES (?, ?, 'created', NULL, 'todo', ?, ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        item_uuid,
                        self.namespace,
                        f"Created {clean_kind}: {clean_title}",
                        str(created_by or "agent"),
                        now_iso,
                    ),
                )
                row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE id = ?", (item_uuid,)).fetchone()
                item_dict = dict(row)
                item_dict["tags"] = clean_tags
                item_dict["evidence"] = []
                return item_dict

    def update_work_item(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        status: Optional[str] = None,
        priority: Optional[str] = None,
        title: Optional[str] = None,
        description: Optional[str] = None,
        note: Optional[str] = None,
        agent_id: str = "agent",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        now_iso = _utc_now_iso()
        with namespace_write_lock(self.namespace):
            with self._connect() as conn:
                conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
                item = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, item_id)
                if not item:
                    raise ValueError(self._not_found_error_message(conn, item_id))

                full_id = item["id"]
                updates: list[str] = []
                params: list[Any] = []
                old_status = item["status"]
                new_status = old_status
                event_type = "updated"

                if status is not None:
                    clean_status = str(status).strip().lower()
                    if clean_status in ("reopened", "reopen"):
                        new_status = "todo"
                        updates.append("status = ?")
                        params.append("todo")
                        event_type = "reopened"
                        if item["completed_at"]:
                            updates.append("completed_at = NULL")
                        if item["completed_by"]:
                            updates.append("completed_by = NULL")
                    elif clean_status not in ("todo", "in_progress", "blocked", "done", "cancelled"):
                        raise ValueError(f"Invalid status '{status}'. Must be todo, in_progress, blocked, done, cancelled, or reopened.")
                    else:
                        new_status = clean_status
                        updates.append("status = ?")
                        params.append(new_status)
                        event_type = new_status
                        if new_status == "in_progress" and not item["started_at"]:
                            updates.append("started_at = ?")
                            params.append(now_iso)

                if priority is not None:
                    clean_priority = str(priority).strip().lower()
                    if clean_priority not in ("low", "medium", "high", "critical"):
                        raise ValueError(f"Invalid priority '{priority}'.")
                    updates.append("priority = ?")
                    params.append(clean_priority)

                if title is not None:
                    guard_t = guard_ingest(title, local_only=bool(item["local_only"]))
                    if guard_t.rejected:
                        raise ValueError(f"Title rejected: {guard_t.reason}")
                    updates.append("title = ?")
                    params.append(guard_t.content.strip())
                    if guard_t.local_only and not item["local_only"]:
                        updates.append("local_only = 1")

                if description is not None:
                    if str(description).strip():
                        guard_d = guard_ingest(description, local_only=bool(item["local_only"]))
                        if guard_d.rejected:
                            raise ValueError(f"Description rejected: {guard_d.reason}")
                        updates.append("description = ?")
                        params.append(guard_d.content.strip())
                        if guard_d.local_only and not item["local_only"]:
                            updates.append("local_only = 1")
                    else:
                        updates.append("description = ?")
                        params.append("")

                clean_note = None
                if note is not None and str(note).strip():
                    guard_n = guard_ingest(note)
                    clean_note = guard_n.content.strip()
                    if guard_n.local_only and not item["local_only"]:
                        updates.append("local_only = 1")

                if updates:
                    params.append(full_id)
                    conn.execute(f"UPDATE work_items SET {', '.join(updates)} WHERE id = ?", params)

                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO work_events
                    (item_id, namespace, event_type, old_status, new_status, note, agent_id, timestamp)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        full_id,
                        self.namespace,
                        event_type,
                        old_status if status is not None else None,
                        new_status if status is not None else None,
                        clean_note,
                        str(agent_id or "agent"),
                        now_iso,
                    ),
                )
                updated_row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE id = ?", (full_id,)).fetchone()
                res = dict(updated_row)
                res["tags"] = json.loads(res.get("tags") or "[]")
                res["evidence"] = json.loads(res.get("evidence") or "[]")
                return res

    def complete_work_item(
        self,
        item_id: str,
        summary: str,
        evidence: Optional[list[str]] = None,
        note: Optional[str] = None,
        agent_id: str = "agent",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        clean_sum = (summary or "").strip()
        if not clean_sum:
            raise ValueError("A detailed summary of what was completed is required to mark a work item done.")

        guard_s = guard_ingest(clean_sum)
        if guard_s.rejected:
            raise ValueError(f"Summary rejected: {guard_s.reason}")

        now_iso = _utc_now_iso()
        valid_ev: list[str] = [str(x).strip() for x in (evidence or []) if str(x).strip()]

        clean_note = None
        guard_n = None
        if note is not None and str(note).strip():
            guard_n = guard_ingest(note)
            clean_note = guard_n.content.strip()
        else:
            clean_note = guard_s.content.strip()

        with namespace_write_lock(self.namespace):
            with self._connect() as conn:
                conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
                item = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, item_id)
                if not item:
                    raise ValueError(self._not_found_error_message(conn, item_id))

                full_id = item["id"]
                old_status = item["status"]
                is_priv = bool(item["local_only"] or guard_s.local_only or (guard_n and guard_n.local_only))
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    UPDATE work_items
                    SET status = 'done',
                        completed_at = ?,
                        completed_by = ?,
                        summary = ?,
                        evidence = ?,
                        local_only = ?
                    WHERE id = ?
                    """,
                    (
                        now_iso,
                        str(agent_id or "agent"),
                        guard_s.content.strip(),
                        json.dumps(valid_ev),
                        1 if is_priv else 0,
                        full_id,
                    ),
                )
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO work_events
                    (item_id, namespace, event_type, old_status, new_status, note, agent_id, timestamp)
                    VALUES (?, ?, 'completed', ?, 'done', ?, ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (
                        full_id,
                        self.namespace,
                        old_status,
                        clean_note,
                        str(agent_id or "agent"),
                        now_iso,
                    ),
                )
                updated_row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE id = ?", (full_id,)).fetchone()
                res = dict(updated_row)
                res["tags"] = json.loads(res.get("tags") or "[]")
                res["evidence"] = valid_ev
                return res

    def _deserialize_work_item_row(self, row: sqlite3.Row | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = dict(row)
        d["title"] = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(d.get("title"))
        d["description"] = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(d.get("description") or "")
        if d.get("summary"):
            d["summary"] = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(d["summary"])
        d["tags"] = json.loads(d.get("tags") or "[]")
        d["evidence"] = json.loads(d.get("evidence") or "[]")
        d["local_only"] = bool(d.get("local_only"))
        return d

    def _deserialize_work_event_row(self, row: sqlite3.Row | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        d = dict(row)
        if d.get("note"):
            d["note"] = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(d["note"])
        return d

    def get_work_item(self, item_id: str, include_private: bool = True) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            item = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, item_id)
            if not item:
                return None
            if not include_private and item["local_only"]:
                return None
            return self._deserialize_work_item_row(item)

    def list_work_items(
        self,
        status: Optional[str] = None,
        kind: Optional[str] = None,
        parent_id: Optional[str] = None,
        include_private: bool = False,
        limit: int = 50,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        safe_limit = max(1, min(1000, int(limit or 50)))
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            query = "SELECT * FROM work_items WHERE namespace = ?"
            params: list[Any] = [self.namespace]

            if not include_private:
                query += " AND local_only = 0"
            if status:
                clean_status = str(status).strip().lower()
                if clean_status == "open":
                    query += " AND status IN ('todo', 'in_progress', 'blocked')"
                elif clean_status == "closed":
                    query += " AND status IN ('done', 'cancelled')"
                else:
                    query += " AND status = ?"
                    params.append(clean_status)
            if kind:
                query += " AND kind = ?"
                params.append(str(kind).strip().lower())
            if parent_id:
                p_item = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, parent_id)
                p_uuid = p_item["id"] if p_item else parent_id
                query += " AND parent_id = ?"
                params.append(p_uuid)

            query += (
                " ORDER BY CASE priority WHEN 'critical' THEN 1 WHEN 'high' THEN 2 "
                "WHEN 'medium' THEN 3 WHEN 'low' THEN 4 ELSE 5 END, "
                "created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?"
            )
            params.append(safe_limit)

            rows = conn.execute(query, params).fetchall()
            return [self._deserialize_work_item_row(r) for r in rows]

    def get_work_events(self, item_id: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        safe_limit = max(1, min(1000, int(limit or 50)))
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            query = "SELECT * FROM work_events WHERE namespace = ?"
            params: list[Any] = [self.namespace]
            if item_id:
                target = self._resolve_work_item_locked(conn, item_id)
                target_id = target["id"] if target else item_id
                query += " AND item_id = ?"
                params.append(target_id)
            query += " ORDER BY timestamp DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?"
            params.append(safe_limit)
            return [self._deserialize_work_event_row(r) for r in conn.execute(query, params).fetchall()]

    def get_work_item_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        with self._connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    COUNT(*) AS total,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN kind = 'goal' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS goals,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN kind = 'task' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS tasks,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'todo' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS todo,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'in_progress' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS in_progress,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'blocked' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS blocked,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'done' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS done,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'cancelled' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS cancelled
                FROM work_items
                WHERE namespace = ?
                """,
                (self.namespace,),
            ).fetchone()
            if not row:
                return {
                    "total": 0,
                    "by_kind": {"goal": 0, "task": 0},
                    "by_status": {"todo": 0, "in_progress": 0, "blocked": 0, "done": 0, "cancelled": 0},
                }
            return {
                "total": int(row[0]),
                "by_kind": {"goal": int(row[1]), "task": int(row[2])},
                "by_status": {
                    "todo": int(row[3]),
                    "in_progress": int(row[4]),
                    "blocked": int(row[5]),
                    "done": int(row[6]),
                    "cancelled": int(row[7]),
                },
            }

    def get_schema_version(self) -> str:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        with self._connect() as conn:
            try:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM mesh_meta WHERE key = 'schema_version'").fetchone()
                if row:
                    return str(row[0])
            except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                pass
            try:
                row = conn.execute("SELECT version FROM schema_info").fetchone()
                if row:
                    return str(row[0])
            except sqlite3.OperationalError:
                pass
            return "1"

    def get_project_brief(
        self,
        max_tokens: int = 500,
        include_work_items: bool = True,
        include_private: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        open_items = (
            self.list_work_items(status="open", include_private=include_private, limit=10)
            if include_work_items
            else []
        )
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            total_mems = conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?",
                (self.namespace,),
            ).fetchone()[0]

            completed_recent = []
            if include_work_items:
                two_days_ago = datetime.fromtimestamp(
                    datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - 172800, tz=timezone.utc
                ).isoformat()
                c_rows = conn.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT * FROM work_items
                    WHERE namespace = ? AND status = 'done' AND completed_at >= ?
                    ORDER BY completed_at DESC LIMIT 5
                    """,
                    (self.namespace, two_days_ago),
                ).fetchall()
                for r in c_rows:
                    cd = dict(r)
                    cd["tags"] = json.loads(cd.get("tags") or "[]")
                    cd["evidence"] = json.loads(cd.get("evidence") or "[]")
                    completed_recent.append(cd)

            contradictions_count = conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM contradictions WHERE namespace = ? AND resolved = 0",
                (self.namespace,),
            ).fetchone()[0]

            if total_mems == 0 and not open_items and not completed_recent:
                msg = (
                    f"# Project Brief: {self.namespace}\n\n"
                    f"*Namespace state:* `new_or_empty` (empty)\n"
                    f"No memories, goals, or tasks exist yet in this namespace. "
                    f"Use `mesh_learn` to record durable conventions/facts and `mesh_task_create` to track work."
                )
                return {
                    "namespace": self.namespace,
                    "namespace_state": "new_or_empty",
                    "total_memories": 0,
                    "open_tasks_count": 0,
                    "completed_recent_count": 0,
                    "pending_contradictions": 0,
                    "brief_markdown": msg,
                    "estimated_tokens": max(1, len(msg) // 4),
                }

            key_mems = []
            priv_filter = "AND local_only = 0" if not include_private else ""
            km_rows = conn.execute(
                f"""
                SELECT id, content, memory_type, confidence, tags
                FROM memories
                WHERE namespace = ? AND quarantined = 0 {priv_filter}
                  AND memory_type IN ('fact', 'decision', 'process', 'preference')
                ORDER BY confidence DESC, accessed_at DESC LIMIT 5
                """,
                (self.namespace,),
            ).fetchall()
            for r in km_rows:
                dec_c = decrypt_text_if_encrypted(r["content"])
                key_mems.append({
                    "id": r["id"],
                    "content": dec_c[:160] + "..." if len(dec_c) > 160 else dec_c,
                    "type": r["memory_type"],
                })

            lines: list[str] = [
                f"# Project Brief: {self.namespace}",
                f"**Health:** {total_mems} memories | {len(open_items)} open work items | {contradictions_count} pending contradictions",
                "",
            ]

            if open_items:
                lines.append("## Open Goals & Tasks")
                for it in open_items[:5]:
                    p_badge = f"[{it['priority'].upper()}]" if it["priority"] in ("high", "critical") else ""
                    lines.append(f"- **{it['short_id']}** {p_badge} ({it['status']}): {it['title']}")
                lines.append("")

            if completed_recent:
                lines.append("## Recently Completed (Past 48h)")
                for it in completed_recent[:4]:
                    summ = it.get("summary") or "Done"
                    if len(summ) > 120:
                        summ = summ[:117] + "..."
                    lines.append(f"- **{it['short_id']}**: {it['title']} — *{summ}*")
                lines.append("")

            if key_mems:
                lines.append("## Key Project Facts & Decisions")
                for km in key_mems[:4]:
                    extra_hint = ""
                    if km["type"] == "decision":
                        content_low = km["content"].lower()
                        causal_markers = ("because", "since", "due to", "as ", "rationale:", "reason:")
                        if not any(marker in content_low for marker in causal_markers):
                            extra_hint = " *(needs rationale — add 'because <reason>')*"
                    lines.append(f"- [{km['type']}] {km['content']}{extra_hint}")
                lines.append("")

            if contradictions_count > 0:
                lines.append(f"> [!WARNING]\n> {contradictions_count} unresolved contradiction(s) detected. Run `mesh_contradictions` to review.")
                lines.append("")

            full_md = "\n".join(lines).strip()
            budget_chars = max_tokens * 4
            if len(full_md) > budget_chars:
                full_md = full_md[:budget_chars].rsplit("\n", 1)[0]
                full_md += "\n\n*(Brief capped at token budget. Call `mesh_recall` or `mesh_task_list` for full details.)*"

            return {
                "namespace": self.namespace,
                "namespace_state": "active",
                "total_memories": total_mems,
                "open_tasks_count": len(open_items),
                "completed_recent_count": len(completed_recent),
                "pending_contradictions": contradictions_count,
                "key_facts_count": len(key_mems),
                "brief_markdown": full_md,
                "estimated_tokens": max(1, len(full_md) // 4),
            }

    def get_changelog(
        self,
        since: str = "7d",
        format: str = "markdown",
        include_private: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self._ensure_initialized()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        since_str = str(since or "7d").strip()
        rel_match = re.match(r"^(\d+)\s*([smhdw])$", since_str.lower())
        if rel_match:
            val, unit = int(rel_match.group(1)), rel_match.group(2)
            secs = val * {"s": 1, "m": 60, "h": 3600, "d": 86400, "w": 604800}[unit]
            cutoff_iso = datetime.fromtimestamp(now.timestamp() - secs, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat()
        else:
            cutoff_iso = since_str

        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            priv_filter = "AND local_only = 0" if not include_private else ""

            c_rows = conn.execute(
                f"""
                SELECT * FROM work_items
                WHERE namespace = ? AND status = 'done' AND completed_at >= ? {priv_filter}
                ORDER BY completed_at DESC
                """,
                (self.namespace, cutoff_iso),
            ).fetchall()
            completed_items = []
            for r in c_rows:
                d = dict(r)
                d["tags"] = json.loads(d.get("tags") or "[]")
                d["evidence"] = json.loads(d.get("evidence") or "[]")
                completed_items.append(d)

            d_rows = conn.execute(
                f"""
                SELECT id, content, tags, created_at, source_agent
                FROM memories
                WHERE namespace = ? AND memory_type = 'decision' AND created_at >= ? {priv_filter}
                ORDER BY created_at DESC
                """,
                (self.namespace, cutoff_iso),
            ).fetchall()
            decisions = []
            for r in d_rows:
                decisions.append({
                    "id": r["id"],
                    "content": decrypt_text_if_encrypted(r["content"]),
                    "created_at": r["created_at"],
                    "source_agent": r["source_agent"],
                })

            lines = [
                f"# Project Changelog: {self.namespace}",
                f"**Window:** Since `{since_str}` ({cutoff_iso[:10]}) | **Completed Items:** {len(completed_items)} | **Decisions:** {len(decisions)}",
                "",
            ]

            lines.append("## Completed Work")
            if not completed_items:
                lines.append("*No completed work items recorded in this window.*")
            else:
                for it in completed_items:
                    lines.append(f"### {it['short_id']}: {it['title']}")
                    lines.append(f"- **Completed at:** {it['completed_at'][:19]} by `{it.get('completed_by') or 'agent'}`")
                    if it.get("summary"):
                        lines.append(f"- **Summary:** {it['summary']}")
                    if it.get("evidence"):
                        lines.append(f"- **Evidence:** {', '.join(f'`{e}`' for e in it['evidence'])}")
                    lines.append("")

            lines.append("## Architectural & Design Decisions")
            if not decisions:
                lines.append("*No new architectural decisions recorded in this window.*")
            else:
                for dec in decisions:
                    lines.append(f"- **[{dec['created_at'][:10]}]** {dec['content']} *(by `{dec['source_agent']}`)*")
                lines.append("")

            changelog_md = "\n".join(lines).strip()
            return {
                "namespace": self.namespace,
                "since": since_str,
                "cutoff": cutoff_iso,
                "completed_count": len(completed_items),
                "decisions_count": len(decisions),
                "completed_items": completed_items,
                "decisions": decisions,
                "changelog": changelog_md,
                "changelog_markdown": changelog_md,
            }

    # Method aliases mirroring MCP tool names (Issue A-006 / B-004)
    task_create = create_work_item
    task_update = update_work_item
    task_complete = complete_work_item
    task_list = list_work_items
    task_get = get_work_item
    log = get_changelog


=== FILE: mesh/ui.py ===
"""
mesh/ui.py — Terminal design system for Mesh.

One small, dependency-free module that every command uses to talk to the human
at the keyboard, so the whole CLI looks and feels the same.

Design rules
  - stdlib only and import-cheap (keeps `mesh --help` instant)
  - results go to stdout, status/diagnostics/errors go to stderr (pipes stay clean)
  - colour is a bonus, never required: NO_COLOR, FORCE_COLOR, TERM=dumb, non-TTY
    and legacy 16-colour terminals are all handled
  - Unicode is a bonus, never required: every symbol has an ASCII fallback
    (force with MESH_ASCII=1)
  - never print raw tags like [OK] / [..] / [INPUT]; use one consistent
    vocabulary of symbols and words instead
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import textwrap
import threading
import time
import unicodedata
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Iterator, NoReturn, Optional, Sequence, Union

__all__ = [
    "style", "color_enabled", "unicode_enabled", "interactive", "term_width",
    "sym", "visible_len", "truncate", "wrap", "plural", "relative_time",
    "short_path", "meter", "badge",
    "blank", "out", "err", "header", "section", "rule", "kv", "table", "box",
    "success", "info", "warn", "error", "fail", "step", "hint", "note", "next_steps",
    "readline_safe",
    "spinner", "confirm", "prompt_text", "json_out", "command_hint",
]

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Capability detection
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_ANSI_RE = re.compile(r"\x1b\[[0-9;?]*[A-Za-z]")
_VT_ENABLED = False
_NI_HINT_SHOWN = False
_OUT_LOCK = threading.RLock()
_ACTIVE_SPINNERS: list["_Spinner"] = []


def _flag(name: str) -> bool:
    return os.environ.get(name, "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")


def _enable_windows_vt() -> None:
    """Turn on ANSI escape processing for legacy Windows consoles (best effort, once)."""
    global _VT_ENABLED
    if _VT_ENABLED or os.name != "nt":
        return
    _VT_ENABLED = True
    try:
        import ctypes

        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        for std_handle in (-11, -12):  # stdout, stderr
            handle = kernel32.GetStdHandle(std_handle)
            mode = ctypes.c_uint32()
            if kernel32.GetConsoleMode(handle, ctypes.byref(mode)):
                kernel32.SetConsoleMode(handle, mode.value | 0x0004)
    except Exception:
        pass


def color_enabled(stream: Any = None) -> bool:
    """Colour policy: NO_COLOR wins, then FORCE_COLOR, then 'is this a real terminal?'."""
    stream = stream if stream is not None else sys.stdout
    if os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") or _flag("MESH_NO_COLOR"):
        return False
    if os.environ.get("FORCE_COLOR") or _flag("MESH_FORCE_COLOR"):
        return True
    if os.environ.get("TERM", "").lower() == "dumb":
        return False
    ok = hasattr(stream, "isatty") and stream.isatty()
    if ok:
        _enable_windows_vt()
    return ok


def _color_depth() -> int:
    """24 = truecolor, 8 = 256 colours, 4 = basic 16 colours."""
    colorterm = os.environ.get("COLORTERM", "").lower()
    if colorterm in ("truecolor", "24bit"):
        return 24
    if os.name == "nt" or os.environ.get("WT_SESSION"):
        return 24
    if os.environ.get("TERM_PROGRAM") in ("iTerm.app", "vscode", "WezTerm", "ghostty"):
        return 24
    term = os.environ.get("TERM", "").lower()
    if "256" in term or "kitty" in term or "alacritty" in term:
        return 8
    return 4


def unicode_enabled(stream: Any = None) -> bool:
    if _flag("MESH_ASCII"):
        return False
    stream = stream if stream is not None else sys.stdout
    enc = getattr(stream, "encoding", None) or "ascii"
    try:
        "✓─│╭·→█░▲●".encode(enc)
        return True
    except (UnicodeEncodeError, LookupError):
        return False


def interactive() -> bool:
    """True when a human is plausibly watching (both stdin and stdout are terminals)."""
    return bool(
        hasattr(sys.stdin, "isatty") and sys.stdin.isatty()
        and hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty") and sys.stdout.isatty()
    )


def term_width(maximum: int = 110, minimum: int = 48) -> int:
    cols = shutil.get_terminal_size((88, 24)).columns
    return max(minimum, min(cols, maximum))


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Styling
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# name -> ((r, g, b), basic-16 SGR foreground code)
_PALETTE: dict[str, tuple[tuple[int, int, int], int]] = {
    "brand":  ((16, 185, 129), 32),    # emerald
    "info":   ((96, 165, 250), 34),    # blue
    "accent": ((167, 139, 250), 35),   # violet
    "warn":   ((245, 158, 11), 33),    # amber
    "error":  ((248, 113, 113), 31),   # red
    "muted":  ((139, 149, 167), 90),   # grey
}
_ATTRS = {"bold": "1", "dim": "2", "italic": "3", "underline": "4"}


def _rgb_to_256(r: int, g: int, b: int) -> int:
    def to6(v: int) -> int:
        return 0 if v < 48 else 1 if v < 115 else min(5, (v - 35) // 40)

    return 16 + 36 * to6(r) + 6 * to6(g) + to6(b)


def _sgr(names: Sequence[str]) -> str:
    depth = _color_depth()
    codes: list[str] = []
    for n in names:
        if n in _ATTRS:
            codes.append(_ATTRS[n])
        elif n in _PALETTE:
            (r, g, b), basic = _PALETTE[n]
            if depth == 24:
                codes.append(f"38;2;{r};{g};{b}")
            elif depth == 8:
                codes.append(f"38;5;{_rgb_to_256(r, g, b)}")
            else:
                codes.append(str(basic))
    return ";".join(codes)


def style(text: Any, *names: str, stream: Any = None) -> str:
    """style("hello", "bold", "brand") — returns plain text when colour is off."""
    s = str(text)
    if not names or not color_enabled(stream):
        return s
    codes = _sgr(names)
    if not codes:
        return s
    return f"\x1b[{codes}m{s}\x1b[0m"


def _estyle(text: Any, *names: str) -> str:
    """style() for text destined to stderr."""
    return style(text, *names, stream=sys.stderr)


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Symbols
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_SYMBOLS_UNI = {
    "ok": "✓", "fail": "✗", "warn": "▲", "info": "●", "pending": "○",
    "arrow": "→", "dot": "·", "bar": "┃", "bullet": "•", "rule": "─",
    "meter_on": "█", "meter_off": "░", "brand": "◆", "prompt": "❯",
    "tl": "╭", "tr": "╮", "bl": "╰", "br": "╯", "v": "│", "h": "─",
    "ellipsis": "…", "q": "?",
}
_SYMBOLS_ASCII = {
    "ok": "+", "fail": "x", "warn": "!", "info": "*", "pending": "o",
    "arrow": "->", "dot": "-", "bar": "|", "bullet": "*", "rule": "-",
    "meter_on": "#", "meter_off": "-", "brand": "*", "prompt": ">",
    "tl": "+", "tr": "+", "bl": "+", "br": "+", "v": "|", "h": "-",
    "ellipsis": "...", "q": "?",
}


def sym(name: str, stream: Any = None) -> str:
    table = _SYMBOLS_UNI if unicode_enabled(stream) else _SYMBOLS_ASCII
    return table.get(name, "")


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Text helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def visible_len(text: str) -> int:
    """Display width of `text` ignoring ANSI codes and counting wide (CJK) glyphs as 2."""
    plain = _ANSI_RE.sub("", text)
    width = 0
    for ch in plain:
        if unicodedata.combining(ch):
            continue
        width += 2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(ch) in ("W", "F") else 1
    return width


def truncate(plain: str, width: int) -> str:
    """Truncate *plain* (un-styled) text to `width` columns with an ellipsis."""
    plain = " ".join(str(plain).split())
    if visible_len(plain) <= width:
        return plain
    ell = sym("ellipsis")
    budget = max(1, width - visible_len(ell))
    out_chars: list[str] = []
    used = 0
    for ch in plain:
        w = 2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(ch) in ("W", "F") else 1
        if used + w > budget:
            break
        out_chars.append(ch)
        used += w
    return "".join(out_chars).rstrip() + ell


def wrap(text: str, width: int, indent: str = "", first_indent: Optional[str] = None, max_lines: Optional[int] = None) -> list[str]:
    """Word-wrap plain text (collapsing whitespace). Optionally cap at `max_lines` with an ellipsis."""
    flat = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
    if not flat:
        return [first_indent if first_indent is not None else indent]
    lines = textwrap.wrap(
        flat,
        width=max(10, width),
        initial_indent=first_indent if first_indent is not None else indent,
        subsequent_indent=indent,
        break_long_words=True,
        break_on_hyphens=False,
    )
    if max_lines is not None and len(lines) > max_lines:
        lines = lines[:max_lines]
        lines[-1] = truncate(lines[-1], max(10, width - 1)).rstrip(sym("ellipsis")) + sym("ellipsis")
    return lines


def plural(n: int, singular: str, plural_form: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    return f"{n} {singular if n == 1 else (plural_form or singular + 's')}"


def _parse_dt(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        dt = value
    else:
        raw = str(value or "").strip()
        if not raw or raw.lower() in ("never", "error", "none"):
            return None
        try:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        except ValueError:
            return None
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return dt


def relative_time(value: Any, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> str:
    """'just now', '5m ago', '3h ago', '2d ago', '3w ago', or 'Mar 4, 2025'."""
    dt = _parse_dt(value)
    if dt is None:
        return "never" if str(value or "").strip().lower() == "never" else ""
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    secs = (now - dt).total_seconds()
    if secs < 0:
        return "just now"
    if secs < 45:
        return "just now"
    if secs < 3600:
        return f"{int(secs // 60) or 1}m ago"
    if secs < 86400:
        return f"{int(secs // 3600)}h ago"
    if secs < 86400 * 14:
        return f"{int(secs // 86400)}d ago"
    if secs < 86400 * 60:
        return f"{int(secs // (86400 * 7))}w ago"
    return dt.strftime("%b %d, %Y").replace(" 0", " ")


def short_path(path: Any) -> str:
    """Replace the home directory with ~ for compact display."""
    p = str(path)
    try:
        home = str(Path.home())
        if p == home:
            return "~"
        if p.startswith(home + os.sep):
            return "~" + p[len(home):]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return p


def meter(fraction: float, width: int = 8, stream: Any = None) -> str:
    """Compact bar like ████░░░░ coloured by strength (green / amber / grey)."""
    f = max(0.0, min(1.0, float(fraction or 0.0)))
    filled = int(round(f * width))
    color = "brand" if f >= 0.7 else ("warn" if f >= 0.4 else "muted")
    on, off = sym("meter_on"), sym("meter_off")
    return style(on * filled, color, stream=stream) + style(off * (width - filled), "muted", "dim", stream=stream)


def badge(text: str, color: str = "muted") -> str:
    """A small status word: coloured when possible, [bracketed] when not."""
    if color_enabled():
        return style(text, color, "bold")
    return f"[{text}]"


def command_hint(cmd: str) -> str:
    """Render a shell command so it stands out inside a sentence."""
    return style(cmd, "info") if color_enabled() else f"`{cmd}`"


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Output primitives
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

IND = "  "


def out(text: str = "") -> None:
    print(text)


def err(text: str = "") -> None:
    print(text, file=sys.stderr)


def blank() -> None:
    print()


def rule(width: Optional[int] = None, label: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    width = width or min(term_width(), 64)
    ch = sym("rule")
    if label:
        lead = f"{ch}{ch} {label} "
        return style(lead + ch * max(2, width - visible_len(lead)), "muted", "dim")
    return style(ch * width, "muted", "dim")


def header(title: str, *meta: str) -> None:
    """
      ◆ Title   meta · meta
      ──────────────────────
    """
    print()
    meta_s = ""
    metas = [m for m in meta if m]
    if metas:
        meta_s = "  " + style(f" {sym('dot')} ".join(metas), "muted")
    print(f"{IND}{style(sym('brand'), 'brand')} {style(title, 'bold')}{meta_s}")
    print(f"{IND}{rule(min(term_width() - 4, 64))}")


def section(title: str) -> None:
    print()
    print(f"{IND}{style(title.upper(), 'muted', 'bold')}")


def kv(rows: Iterable[tuple[str, Any]], key_width: Optional[int] = None, indent: str = IND) -> None:
    """Aligned 'key   value' rows. Rows whose value is None are skipped."""
    items = [(k, v) for k, v in rows if v is not None]
    if not items:
        return
    kw = key_width or max(len(k) for k, _ in items) + 2
    for k, v in items:
        print(f"{indent}{style(k.ljust(kw), 'muted')}{v}")


Cell = Union[str, tuple]


def _cell_parts(cell: Cell) -> tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
    if isinstance(cell, tuple):
        return str(cell[0]), tuple(cell[1:])
    return str(cell), ()


def table(
    headers: Sequence[str],
    rows: Sequence[Sequence[Cell]],
    *,
    aligns: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None,
    flex: Optional[int] = None,
    indent: str = IND,
    gap: int = 2,
) -> None:
    """
    Borderless table. Cells are plain strings or (text, style, style...) tuples.
    Column `flex` absorbs/shrinks to keep the table inside the terminal.
    Cells passed as plain str that already contain ANSI codes are never truncated.
    """
    ncols = len(headers)
    aligns = list(aligns or ["l"] * ncols)
    parts = [[_cell_parts(c) for c in row] for row in rows]
    widths = [visible_len(h) for h in headers]
    for row in parts:
        for i, (text, _) in enumerate(row):
            widths[i] = max(widths[i], visible_len(text))

    if flex is not None:
        avail = term_width() - len(indent) - gap * (ncols - 1)
        other = sum(w for i, w in enumerate(widths) if i != flex)
        widths[flex] = max(12, min(widths[flex], avail - other))

    def render(text: str, styles: tuple[str, ...], i: int) -> str:
        plain_has_ansi = bool(_ANSI_RE.search(text))
        shown = text if plain_has_ansi else truncate(text, widths[i])
        pad = widths[i] - visible_len(shown)
        body = style(shown, *styles) if styles else shown
        if i == ncols - 1 and aligns[i] != "r":
            return body
        if aligns[i] == "r":
            return " " * max(0, pad) + body
        return body + " " * max(0, pad)

    head_cells = []
    for i, h in enumerate(headers):
        last_left = i == ncols - 1 and aligns[i] != "r"
        txt = h.upper() if last_left else _pad_plain(h.upper(), widths[i], aligns[i])
        head_cells.append(style(txt, "muted", "bold"))
    print(f"{indent}{(' ' * gap).join(head_cells).rstrip()}")
    print(f"{indent}{style(sym('rule') * min(sum(widths) + gap * (ncols - 1), term_width() - len(indent)), 'muted', 'dim')}")
    for row in parts:
        cells = [render(text, styles, i) for i, (text, styles) in enumerate(row)]
        print(indent + (" " * gap).join(cells).rstrip())


def _pad_plain(text: str, width: int, align: str) -> str:
    pad = max(0, width - visible_len(text))
    return " " * pad + text if align == "r" else text + " " * pad


def box(lines: Sequence[str], title: Optional[str] = None, color: str = "brand", indent: str = IND) -> None:
    """Rounded panel. `lines` may contain ANSI styling."""
    inner = max([visible_len(l) for l in lines] + [visible_len(title or "") + 2, 20])
    inner = min(inner, term_width() - len(indent) - 4)
    tl, tr, bl, br, v, h = (sym(n) for n in ("tl", "tr", "bl", "br", "v", "h"))
    if title:
        top = style(tl + h + " ", color) + style(title, "bold", color) + style(" " + h * max(1, inner - visible_len(title) - 1) + tr, color)
    else:
        top = style(tl + h * (inner + 2) + tr, color)
    print(f"{indent}{top}")
    for l in lines:
        pad = max(0, inner - visible_len(l))
        print(f"{indent}{style(v, color)} {l}{' ' * pad} {style(v, color)}")
    print(f"{indent}{style(bl + h * (inner + 2) + br, color)}")


# ── status lines ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def _status_line(symbol_name: str, color: str, msg: str, detail: Optional[str], stream: Any) -> None:
    st = (lambda t, *n: style(t, *n, stream=stream))
    glyph = st(sym(symbol_name, stream), color, "bold")
    with _OUT_LOCK:
        _clear_spinner_line()
        print(f"{IND}{glyph} {msg}", file=stream)
        if detail:
            for line in str(detail).splitlines():
                print(f"{IND}  {st(line, 'muted')}", file=stream)


def success(msg: str, detail: Optional[str] = None, *, stream: Any = None) -> None:
    _status_line("ok", "brand", msg, detail, stream or sys.stdout)


def info(msg: str, detail: Optional[str] = None, *, stream: Any = None) -> None:
    _status_line("info", "info", msg, detail, stream or sys.stdout)


def step(msg: str, detail: Optional[str] = None, *, stream: Any = None) -> None:
    s = stream or sys.stdout
    _status_line("pending", "muted", style(msg, "muted", stream=s), detail, s)


def warn(msg: str, hint_text: Optional[str] = None, *, stdout: bool = False) -> None:
    stream = sys.stdout if stdout else sys.stderr
    _status_line("warn", "warn", msg, None, stream)
    if hint_text:
        hint(hint_text, stream=stream)


def error(msg: str, hint_text: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    _status_line("fail", "error", style(msg, "error", stream=sys.stderr), None, sys.stderr)
    if hint_text:
        hint(hint_text, stream=sys.stderr)


def fail(msg: str, hint_text: Optional[str] = None, code: int = 1) -> NoReturn:
    """Print a styled error to stderr and exit."""
    error(msg, hint_text)
    sys.exit(code)


def note(msg: str) -> None:
    """Quiet one-line diagnostic on stderr (e.g. 'falling back to local database')."""
    with _OUT_LOCK:
        _clear_spinner_line()
        print(f"{IND}{style(msg, 'muted', stream=sys.stderr)}", file=sys.stderr)


def hint(text: str, stream: Any = None) -> None:
    stream = stream or sys.stdout
    arrow = style(sym("arrow", stream), "muted", stream=stream)
    with _OUT_LOCK:
        _clear_spinner_line()
        print(f"{IND}  {arrow} {style(text, 'muted', stream=stream)}", file=stream)


def next_steps(items: Sequence[tuple[str, str]], title: str = "Next") -> None:
    """Footer listing suggested follow-up commands: [(command, description), ...]."""
    if not items:
        return
    print()
    print(f"{IND}{style(title, 'muted', 'bold')}")
    cw = max(len(c) for c, _ in items) + 2
    for cmd, desc in items:
        print(f"{IND}  {style(cmd.ljust(cw), 'info')}{style(desc, 'muted')}")


def json_out(data: Any) -> None:
    print(json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str, ensure_ascii=False))


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Interaction
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def _readline_safe(prompt: str) -> str:
    """
    Wrap ANSI sequences in \\001/\\002 so GNU readline/libedit computes the prompt width correctly.
    Importing readline also gives the user arrow-key editing and history in prompts.
    When readline isn't available the markers would print literally, so we leave the prompt alone.
    """
    if os.name == "nt":
        return prompt
    try:
        # Imported for its side effect: enables arrow-key editing and history.
        # `__import__` keeps linters from flagging an otherwise "unused" import.
        __import__("readline")
    except ImportError:
        return prompt
    return re.sub(r"(\x1b\[[0-9;]*m)", "\001\\1\002", prompt)


readline_safe = _readline_safe


def prompt_text(label: str, default: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    """Ask for a line of free text. Raises EOFError/KeyboardInterrupt like input()."""
    suffix = f" {style('(' + default + ')', 'muted')}" if default else ""
    q = style(sym("q"), "info", "bold")
    with _spinners_paused():
        raw = input(_readline_safe(f"{IND}{q} {style(label, 'bold')}{suffix}{style(' ' + sym('arrow') + ' ', 'muted')}")).strip()
    return raw or (default or "")


def confirm(question: str, *, default: bool = False, assume_yes: bool = False, detail: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
    """
    Styled y/n prompt. Returns the default-False in non-interactive contexts and tells
    the user how to proceed instead of failing silently.
    """
    if assume_yes or _flag("MESH_AUTO_APPROVE"):
        return True
    if _flag("MESH_NON_INTERACTIVE") or not interactive():
        global _NI_HINT_SHOWN
        warn(f"Needs your approval: {' '.join(question.split())}")
        if not _NI_HINT_SHOWN:
            _NI_HINT_SHOWN = True
            hint("This isn't an interactive terminal. Re-run in one, or pass --yes to approve.", stream=sys.stderr)
        return False
    q = style(sym("q"), "info", "bold")
    choices = style("[Y/n]" if default else "[y/N]", "muted")
    with _spinners_paused():
        if detail:
            print(f"{IND}{q} {style(question, 'bold')}")
            print(f"{IND}  {style(detail, 'muted')}")
            prompt = f"{IND}  {choices} "
        else:
            prompt = f"{IND}{q} {style(question, 'bold')} {choices} "
        try:
            ans = input(_readline_safe(prompt)).strip().lower()
        except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt, OSError):
            print()
            return False
    if not ans:
        return default
    return ans in ("y", "yes")


def _clear_spinner_line() -> None:
    """Erase a spinner frame before anything else prints (call while holding _OUT_LOCK)."""
    if any(s.animating for s in _ACTIVE_SPINNERS):
        sys.stderr.write("\r\x1b[K")
        sys.stderr.flush()


@contextmanager
def _spinners_paused() -> Iterator[None]:
    """Stop spinner animation while the user is typing an answer, then resume."""
    paused = [s for s in _ACTIVE_SPINNERS if s.running and not s._paused.is_set()]
    with _OUT_LOCK:
        for s in paused:
            s._pause()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        for s in paused:
            s._resume()


class _Spinner:
    FRAMES_UNI = "⠋⠙⠹⠸⠼⠴⠦⠧⠇⠏"
    FRAMES_ASCII = "|/-\\"

    def __init__(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.message = message
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._paused = threading.Event()
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None
        self._drawn = False
        self._active = (
            hasattr(sys.stderr, "isatty") and sys.stderr.isatty()
            and color_enabled(sys.stderr)
            and not _flag("MESH_NO_SPINNER")
            and os.environ.get("TERM", "").lower() != "dumb"
        )
        self._start = time.monotonic()

    @property
    def running(self) -> bool:
        return self._thread is not None and not self._stop.is_set()

    @property
    def animating(self) -> bool:
        return self._thread is not None and self._drawn and not self._paused.is_set() and not self._stop.is_set()

    def update(self, message: str) -> None:
        self.message = message

    def _pause(self) -> None:
        self._paused.set()
        sys.stderr.write("\r\x1b[K\x1b[?25h")  # clear the frame, show the cursor for typing
        sys.stderr.flush()
        self._drawn = False

    def _resume(self) -> None:
        self._paused.clear()
        with _OUT_LOCK:
            sys.stderr.write("\x1b[?25l")
            sys.stderr.flush()

    def _run(self) -> None:
        frames = self.FRAMES_UNI if unicode_enabled(sys.stderr) else self.FRAMES_ASCII
        i = 0
        while not self._stop.wait(0.08):
            if self._paused.is_set() or time.monotonic() - self._start < 0.25:  # don't flash for fast work
                continue
            with _OUT_LOCK:
                if self._paused.is_set() or self._stop.is_set():
                    continue
                frame = _estyle(frames[i % len(frames)], "brand")
                sys.stderr.write(f"\r{IND}{frame} {_estyle(self.message, 'muted')}\x1b[K")
                sys.stderr.flush()
                self._drawn = True
            i += 1

    def __enter__(self) -> "_Spinner":
        if self._active:
            sys.stderr.write("\x1b[?25l")  # hide cursor
            _ACTIVE_SPINNERS.append(self)
            self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name="mesh-spinner", daemon=True)
            self._thread.start()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
        if self._thread is not None:
            self._stop.set()
            self._thread.join(timeout=0.5)
            with _OUT_LOCK:
                if self in _ACTIVE_SPINNERS:
                    _ACTIVE_SPINNERS.remove(self)
                sys.stderr.write("\r\x1b[K\x1b[?25h")  # clear line, show cursor
                sys.stderr.flush()
            self._thread = None

    @property
    def elapsed(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self._start


def spinner(message: str) -> _Spinner:
    """`with ui.spinner("Searching…") as sp:` — animates on stderr only for real terminals."""
    return _Spinner(message)

=== FILE: mesh/version.py ===
"""
mesh/version.py — Package version metadata, PEP 440 version comparison, and opt-in update checker.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
import urllib.request
from typing import Optional

VERSION = "0.8.2"

_PEP440_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*v?(?P<release>\d+(?:\.\d+)*)"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<pre_type>a|alpha|b|beta|c|rc|pre|preview)[-._]?(?P<pre_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<post_type>post|rev|r)[-._]?(?P<post_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<dev_type>dev)[-._]?(?P<dev_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:\+[A-Za-z0-9._-]+)?\s*$",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

_PRE_TYPE_RANK = {
    "a": -3,
    "alpha": -3,
    "b": -2,
    "beta": -2,
    "c": -1,
    "rc": -1,
    "pre": -1,
    "preview": -1,
}

_UPDATE_CHECK_DONE = False
_UPDATE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def parse_version_tuple(version_str: Optional[str]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    """
    Parse a version string into a PEP 440-aware comparable integer tuple:
      `(major, minor, patch, pre_rank, pre_num, post_rank, post_num, dev_rank, dev_num)`

    Sorting order guarantees:
      `0.6.2.dev1 < 0.6.2a1 < 0.6.2b1 < 0.6.2rc1 < 0.6.2 < 0.6.2.post1 < 0.6.3.dev1 < 0.6.3 < 0.7.0 < 1.0.0`
    The first three elements are always `(major, minor, patch)`.
    """
    if not version_str or not isinstance(version_str, str):
        return (0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    m = _PEP440_RE.match(version_str)
    if not m:
        nums = [int(x) for x in re.findall(r"\d+", version_str)[:3]]
        while len(nums) < 3:
            nums.append(0)
        return (nums[0], nums[1], nums[2], 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    rel_parts = [int(x) for x in m.group("release").split(".")[:3]]
    while len(rel_parts) < 3:
        rel_parts.append(0)
    major, minor, patch = rel_parts[0], rel_parts[1], rel_parts[2]

    pre_type = (m.group("pre_type") or "").lower()
    pre_num_raw = m.group("pre_num")
    post_num_raw = m.group("post_num")
    # Detect the *matched optional group*, not the presence of letters anywhere
    # in the string. `re.search("r", ...)` also matched "rc", "pre", "+arch"
    # and local-version labels, silently turning releases into post-releases.
    has_post = m.group("post_type") is not None
    has_dev = m.group("dev_type") is not None
    dev_num_raw = m.group("dev_num")

    if pre_type:
        pre_rank = _PRE_TYPE_RANK.get(pre_type, -1)
        pre_num = int(pre_num_raw) if pre_num_raw is not None else 0
    elif has_dev and not has_post:
        # Pure `.devN` release sorts before alpha (`-3`)
        pre_rank = -4
        pre_num = 0
    else:
        pre_rank = 0
        pre_num = 0

    if has_post:
        post_rank = 1
        post_num = int(post_num_raw) if post_num_raw is not None else 0
    else:
        post_rank = 0
        post_num = 0

    if has_dev:
        dev_rank = -1
        dev_num = int(dev_num_raw) if dev_num_raw is not None else 0
    else:
        dev_rank = 0
        dev_num = 0

    return (major, minor, patch, pre_rank, pre_num, post_rank, post_num, dev_rank, dev_num)


def is_version_compatible(file_version: Optional[str], current_version: str = VERSION) -> bool:
    """
    Verify export schema compatibility using the release segment `(major, minor, patch)`.
    - Missing/empty file_version is rejected.
    - Major versions must match (`0.x` vs `0.y`, or `1.x` vs `1.y`).
    - During `0.x`, file minor version cannot exceed current minor version (e.g. `0.7.0` file on `0.6.3` fails).
    """
    if not file_version or not isinstance(file_version, str) or not file_version.strip():
        return False
    if not re.search(r"\d", file_version):
        return False
    f_maj, f_min, _ = parse_version_tuple(file_version)[:3]
    c_maj, c_min, _ = parse_version_tuple(current_version)[:3]
    if f_maj != c_maj:
        return False
    if c_maj == 0 and f_min > c_min:
        return False
    return True


def check_for_updates(force: bool = False, blocking: bool = False) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Strictly opt-in PyPI update check (`force=True` or `MESH_CHECK_UPDATES=1`).
    Never contacts PyPI when `MESH_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1` or by default.
    """
    global _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_UPDATE_CHECK", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return None
    opt_in = force or os.environ.get("MESH_CHECK_UPDATES", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
    if not opt_in:
        return None
    with _UPDATE_LOCK:
        if _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE and not force:
            return None
        _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE = True

    def _fetch() -> Optional[str]:
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                "https://pypi.org/pypi/mesh-context-layer/json",
                headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": f"mesh-context-layer/{VERSION}"},
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=2.0) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    latest = str(data.get("info", {}).get("version", "")).strip()
                    if latest and parse_version_tuple(latest) > parse_version_tuple(VERSION):
                        try:
                            from . import ui

                            ui.hint(
                                f"Update available: v{VERSION} → v{latest}. "
                                "Run `pip install -U mesh-context-layer`.",
                                stream=sys.stderr,
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            sys.stderr.write(
                                f"[Mesh] Update available: v{VERSION} -> v{latest} "
                                "(run `pip install -U mesh-context-layer`)\n"
                            )
                        return latest
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    if blocking:
        return _fetch()
    threading.Thread(target=_fetch, name="mesh-update-check", daemon=True).start()
    return None
